# 第 19 章　成为昨日的明天

*3.7 版本主视觉　·　图源：米游社*

> As Tomorrow Became Yesterday　·　成为昨日的明天

## 158　Dawn, Caress the Distant Lands Once More　晨曦啊，再抚映遥远地平

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Aedes Elysiae　|　角色：Cyrene; Trailblazer; March 7th; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Gods, Sound the Anthem of Creation'' completed

### Story Recap

**(Trailblazer):** As I bid farewell to Hysilens at the Vortex of Genesis, I noticed something strange about the "Time" Coreflame, and was immediately dragged into the memoria tide of "Oblivion" by "Evernight"... She was once the hidden power that March manifested by sacrificing all of her memories, yet now she harbors other secret intentions in her heart.

**(Trailblazer):** Meanwhile, Dan Heng warped back to the Express via a spirit warp jump, only to discover a Memosnatcher invasion. Everyone then realized that the Remembrance's forces had already permeated Amphoreus and blocked outside intervention... To establish contact with me, Dan Heng was determined to return to Amphoreus immediately.

**(Trailblazer):** After waking up in the "False Trailblaze," I managed to escape with help from Cyrene and Sunday. Unfortunately, "Evernight" held absolute control, causing me to fall into the memoria tide once again... However, Black Swan took this opportunity to quietly approach "Evernight."

**(Trailblazer):** Dan Heng returned to Amphoreus and beckoned the heroic spirit of Phainon with divine blood. The Destruction, descending from the sky, pierced through the Memory Zone and discovered the most hidden corner of the world. In pursuit of "Evernight," Dan Heng arrived at the charred remnants of the Grove of Epiphany.

**(Trailblazer):** In the Grove, where memories became disjointed, Dan Heng received help from "Dan Feng" within his memories and completed his farewell to the past. In their final moments, the "Earth" demigod Terravox entrusted all their duty and authority to Dan Heng. With this newfound strength, Dan Heng found me within the memoria tide.

**(Trailblazer):** We entered the nameless Titan's great tomb and discovered its secret... across the thirty million cycles of Eternal Recurrence, every incarnation of "Cyrene" had come here, offering herself to the Remembrance. With Black Swan's help, we found "Evernight" in the depths of the great tomb.

**(Trailblazer):** When all seemed lost, "Evernight" was captured by March's Camera and trapped within the memory maze of Aedes Elysiae. After several lengthy conversations, March and "Evernight" reached an agreement... "Evernight" returned March's memories, and March finally returned to our side.

**(Trailblazer):** With every condition for the Era Nova fulfilled, we returned to the depths of the great tomb and initiated the final Era Nova Ceremony...

### Talk to Cyrene at the beginning of the new world

> *A visitor from beyond the sky shoulders the world's destiny, writing a new beginning for Amphoreus through "Trailblaze." Beyond the Aedes Elysiae lies a new era that everyone wishes for. Now that you have arrived at the Dawn, (Trailblazer), are you ready to answer the world's summon?*

[Recap] Beyond the curtains of Aedes Elysiae lurked a new world, free from the black tide's torment. Thirty million cycles of Remembrance had changed Cyrene's appearance, and she asked if you're ready to answer the call of the world.

*(Talk to Cyrene)*

**Cyrene:** This view hasn't changed at all, has it?

> **昔涟：**嗯。和记忆中一模一样。

**Cyrene:** The hometown in your memories never changes, but when you return to your senses, you realize that fate has already stolen the time away.

**Cyrene:** Welcome to the trail of "Genesis," (Trailblazer).

- ▸ Cyrene? You look...

**Cyrene:** Seems like the weight of "memories" from thirty million cycles really made me grow up.

**Cyrene:** But if I keep growing, I might be taller than you, right, (Trailblazer)? Then I'm really looking forward to that.

- ▸ Standing here brings back many memories.

**Cyrene:** Me too. Thirty million departures. Thirty million farewells.

> **昔涟：**我听不见他的声音。那里没有为他绽放的花，也没有属于他的星星。

**Cyrene:** Those heavy "memories" will all become the soil upon which the new world shall bud, and this time... We will make it bloom with a different flower.

> **昔涟：**白厄…是唯一一个，没能抵达新世界的人。

- ▸ The trail of "Genesis"?

**Cyrene:** Indeed. The Scepter's extrapolations have reached their end, but the process of "Genesis" has only just begun.

> **昔涟：**你看，那道照彻树庭的金光，依然在天地间徘徊。就像翁法罗斯的神谕。

**Cyrene:** The path ahead will no longer rely on emotionless programs. It'll be decided by the will of humankind.

> **昔涟：**那一定是他留下的指引，指向「毁灭」…最深的黑夜。

**Cyrene:** Before you arrived, I kept wondering... beyond the veil of Aedes Elysiae, what kind of Amphoreus is being born?

> **昔涟：**还记得吗？上一次启程时，也是在这里，我问过开拓者……

**Cyrene:** I can see fragments of it when I close my eyes... The friends who walked alongside us have all moved on toward their respective destinies.

**Cyrene:** Some turning into pillars of the world, supporting the Sky and the Ocean. Others sowing Reason, laying down the Law, and with gentle hands lifting newborns into the light.

> **昔涟：**那时候，你还在为「负世」的职责而烦恼，思考自己该扮演什么样的角色。但现在，所有人都给出了回答……

**Cyrene:** Death and Strife are no longer feared by people, and Trickery has become the innocent games of children.

> **昔涟：**史诗中的「英雄」，只是在每一个被世界需要的场合，恰到好处地出现在那里。

**Cyrene:** All beings living ordinary and peaceful lives within the net woven by Romance and Passage.

> **昔涟：**正如你的到来，让翁法罗斯的命运再度开始转动。

**Cyrene:** There's no black tide, no Flame-Chase, and no Destruction. The world on the other side of the sea is one bathed in sunlight.

> **昔涟：**一个人的性格，就是他/她的命运。

- ▸ It's a world we must protect.
- ▸ It's the dawn that Phainon and everyone had wished for.
- ▸ Peace will reign until the decisive battle arrives.

**Cyrene:** Like a happy and carefree childhood, the "Era Chrysea" in this cycle will last for millennia, until it reaches the moment decided by the prophecy...

> **昔涟：**「我们」…「最好」…都是很美的词呢。

**Cyrene:** Light Calendar 4931, Month of Freedom, the moment belonging to Worldbearing.

> **昔涟：**谢谢你，开拓者。刚才那些话，也是在为我自己加油打气。

**Cyrene:** Are you ready to answer the call of the world, (Trailblazer)?

> **昔涟：**毕竟，在真正为这个故事写下结局前，我也必须鼓起勇气，和你一起出发……

**Cyrene:** Then go, and pen the Throne of Worlds' first stroke... for this world made from countless wishes.

[Recap] A message arrived from the Chronocognitive Anchor: Irontomb reacted to Era Nova. March 7th and Dan Heng, now Titans, spoke with you, promising to meet again at the world's end to face Irontomb together.

**???:** You say that, but it's gonna be ages till we reach Light Calendar 4931.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** I can make use of the crevices of Time, but surely (Trailblazer) and Dan Heng won't have to wait around for a few thousand years... right?

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** That won't be necessary. We can simply sleep, and the "Chronocognitive Anchor" will wake us up.

- ▸ Veil of Evernight? Pillar of Stone?

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** That's right. We've both become a part of the new world. Just waiting for you now.

- ▸ I didn't expect you'd both be here.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** We've both become a part of the new world ages ago. You're the last, slowpoke.

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** Our consciousnesses can converse with each other across the heavens and the earth, possibly due to the influence of the Trailblaze.

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** I am now connected with this Earth, and I can feel the breath of every mountain range...

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** I see that the tallest among them is named "Okhema," and the form of the holy city is already taking shape.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** In other words, your stage is almost set. Do you wanna take a leaf out of Kephale's book and make some prophecies before your nap?

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** But we're not the only ones who have changed.

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** The Chronocognitive Anchor just transmitted a message stating that "Irontomb" has reacted to the "Era Nova."

**Cyrene:** The moment when (Trailblazer) bore the weight of thirty million cycles of "memories"...

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** ...Was probably also when the Scepter devoured thirty million cycles of Destruction.

- ▸ It's bound to happen, and I've long prepared for that.
- ▸ We can't wait any longer. We must stop Irontomb.
- ▸ It's time to take over the burden that Phainon has been carrying...

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** ...

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** Y'know, (Trailblazer)... You don't have to shoulder this world all on your own.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** After someone boards the Express, the word "alone" ceases to exist. So as we reach the end of time, no one will be left to face it alone.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** Dan Heng and I came here to specifically tell you that.

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** Remember, this is world is not a burden you bear alone.

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** It's a journey we Trailblaze together, alongside all of the Express.

- ▸ I know. Thank you, my good friends.
- ▸ No need to remind me. You are always in my mind.
- ▸ Seriously, can't you let me be the protagonist for once?

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** There'll be plenty of time for you to show off in the future.

**Cyrene:** She meant to say that a hero only turns up at the most pivotal moment.

> **昔涟：**被我遗忘了太久，也抗拒了太久的「记忆」。

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** Alright, it's time for Dan Heng and me to go have a super long nap. Look after this world for us, (Trailblazer).

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** The road ahead is long, but we'll be waiting for you at the end.

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** We'll definitely meet again.

**(Trailblazer):** ...

**(Trailblazer):** (Let's take one more look at the other side of the sea before we set off.)

### Head to the dock for one last look at the sea of Aedes Elysiae

*(After previous dialogue)*

**(Trailblazer):** Aedes Elysiae is so quiet.

**Cyrene:** Mmm. Just like how I remember.

**(Trailblazer):** Here we are once more.

[Recap] You and Cyrene gazed out at the sea, while Phainon waited at the world's end. Before writing the ending, Cyrene must set out with you to confront the "memories" she had long forgotten.

*(Approach the dock)*

**(Trailblazer):** (The scent of the black tide is now completely calm.)

**(Trailblazer):** (At last, the starting point of thirty million cycles... has finally welcomed...)

**Cyrene:** ...Its first end.

**Cyrene:** And the final end, right?

**Cyrene:** I can't hear his voice. There are no flowers there that bloom for him, nor is there a star that is his.

**Cyrene:** Phainon... is the only one who didn't reach the new world.

- ▸ He gave everything to obtain this momentary peace.
- ▸ Trust in him, we'll meet again for sure.
- ▸ Phainon awaits us inside the Irontomb.

**Cyrene:** Look. The golden light that shone upon the Grove still meanders between heaven and earth, like the prophecies of Amphoreus.

**Cyrene:** It has to be a sign he left behind. It points towards the Destruction... and the deepest night.

**Cyrene:** Do you still remember what I asked (Trailblazer) at this exact spot the last time we set off?

**Cyrene:** "(Trailblazer), are you ready to become a hero?"

**Cyrene:** You were still burdened by the weight of Worldbearing back then, still unsure of the role you were meant to play. But now... everyone has already given you the answer.

**Cyrene:** The "heroes" of legends were just at the right place at the right time when the world needed a hero.

**Cyrene:** Just like how your arrival kick-started Amphoreus's fate once more.

**Cyrene:** A person's personality is their destiny.

- ▸ Together, we'll all make it to the best possible ending.

**Cyrene:** "We." "Best." ...Those are such beautiful words.

**Cyrene:** Thank you, (Trailblazer). I was also trying to cheer myself up with what I said.

**Cyrene:** After all, before we truly compose the end of this story, I must also pluck up the courage to head off with you...

**Cyrene:** ...

**Cyrene:** And face...

**Cyrene:** A "memory" that I have forgotten for too long... and have denied for too long.

[Recap] In the deepest corner of "memory" lies the Great Tomb of the Nameless Titan, the birthplace of Cyrene. The first Nouspore, Demiurge, resides there.

**(Trailblazer):** It's so dark. What is this place...?

> **开拓者：**好黑。这里是……

**Cyrene:** The Great Tomb of the nameless Titan. The deepest recesses of "memory."

> **昔涟：**无名泰坦大墓，「记忆」最深的角落。

**Cyrene:** Also... the place where "Cyrene" was born.

> **昔涟：**也是，「昔涟」的诞生之地。

- ▸ Weren't you born in Aedes Elysiae?
- ▸ You got your lost memories back?
- ▸ Birthplace? What do you mean...?

**Cyrene:** My apologies, (Trailblazer). The more memories I got back along the way, the more I felt that something wasn't right.

> **昔涟：**对不起呀，开拓者。这一路上，取回的记忆越多，我心中的违和感就越是强烈。

**Cyrene:** There's a sort of anxiety I can't get rid of. It's like when I was back in Aedes Elysiae, when the reflections in the water always made me wonder... which is the real me? The one reflected in the water, or the one standing on the shore?

> **昔涟：**总有一种不安挥之不去。就好像在哀丽秘榭，我望着水面，分不清水中的我和岸边的我，哪一个才是真正的我。

**Cyrene:** But I finally get it now.

> **昔涟：**但现在，我终于明白了。

**Cyrene:** Look, (Trailblazer). The words on that wall are the answer.

> **昔涟：**你看，开拓者。那面墙上的字，就是答案呀。

**???:** "One, two, three, four, five, six, seven"...

> **？？？：**「一、二、三、四、五、六、七」……

**Cyrene:** "Do, re, mi, fa, sol, la, ti"...

> **昔涟：**「哆、徕、咪、发、嗦、啦、嘻」……

**Cyrene:** That rhymeless tune is the lullaby of little fairies.

> **昔涟：**不成调的小曲，是小妖精们的歌谣。

**???:** "As always, I'll read this book to you..."

> **？？？：**「一如既往，我会把这本书念给你听……」

**???:** "That way, it won't just be 'Cyrene's' memories alone."

> **？？？：**「这样一来，它就不再是『昔涟』一个人的回忆。」

**Cyrene:** I've always thought that I was the narrator of the Flame-Chase Journey.

> **昔涟：**我一直以为，自己是逐火之旅的讲述者。

**Cyrene:** But as it turns out, the narrator...

> **昔涟：**但讲故事的人，原来……

**Cyrene:** ...is also the most devoted "audience."

> **昔涟：**也是最专心的「听众」呀。

- ▸ What do you mean...

> **昔涟：**谢谢，开拓者。「每一个你」…听着真温柔呢。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** That's pretty forward of you, you know? Still... thank you, (Trailblazer). That's very reassuring.

> **昔涟：**很主动的发言哦？但…谢谢，开拓者。真令人安心呢。

> **昔涟：**还有，无数个自己。

## 159　Traverse Stars and Stride Cosmos　星路长驰，穹宇远征

> 世界：The Xianzhou Luofu　|　区域：Seat of Divine Foresight　|　角色：Jing Yuan; Feixiao; Yao Guang; Black Swan; The Herta; Welt; Sunday; Himeko; Screwllum; Fourth Mirror; Fu Xuan; Aventurine; Jade; Robin; Serval; Bronya; Seele; Topaz and Numby; Evey; March 7th; Serena; Trailblazer　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Dawn, Caress the Distant Lands Once More'' completed

### Negotiate the campaign and support the Astral Express

[Recap] At the Arbiter-Generals meeting, Jing Yuan volunteered to fight against Irontomb, but the Xianzhou Alliance only permitted the Luofu to send troops. Yao Guang believed that even if Irontomb emerged, Nous won't sit idly by, while Feixiao insisted they must handle Lord Ravagers with caution.

> *The Xianzhou Alliance Oath: "To save future generations from war and bloody dispute." We volunteer for this battle to eliminate our foes, uphold our allies, and protect all life in the cosmos.*

Currently, beyond the sky...

**Jing Yuan:** The interrogation records have been submitted to the Marshal. According to the Ten Lords' Decree, Jingliu and Luocha shall continue to be detained in the Xuling.

> **景元：**审讯卷宗已经上呈元帅。依照十王律令，镜流与罗刹，当继续押往「虚陵」。

**Jing Yuan:** But you, Merlin's Claw, have been reluctant to end the transmission. Do you wish to reminisce with us...

> **景元：**但天击将军迟迟不愿中断通讯，是出于叙旧之心……

**Jing Yuan:** Or is there something else on your mind that you're just desperate to get out?

> **景元：**还是腹中有话，不吐不快呢？

**Feixiao:** You're quite calm, Jing Yuan. The Alliance only authorized the Luofu to be deployed for this Irontomb operation...

> **飞霄：**真沉得住气啊，景元。对铁墓一役，联盟只准许罗浮一舰出兵……

**Feixiao:** They say the Seer Strategist's wisdom shines like a beacon. So why can't you see things clearly now?

> **飞霄：**都说戎韬将军智光昭昭。这会儿怎么看不清局势了？

**Yao Guang:** Look who's talking. I didn't vote against it, did I?

> **爻光：**瞧你说的，我也没投反对票呀。

**Yao Guang:** But with enemies all around us, even a child knows the Marshal needs to keep a few Xianzhou ships in reserve, just in case.

> **爻光：**可大敌环伺，小孩都知道元帅要留几艘仙舟在后方，以备不时之需。

**Yao Guang:** Whoever volunteers first is the Marshal's pick. If you have to ask, I'd say this outcome plays right into Jing Yuan's hands.

> **爻光：**谁先请缨，谁就是元帅的选择。我看——这结果正中景元下怀呢。

**Feixiao:** Like it or not, there's no way we're letting the Luofu take the lead in this battle.

> **飞霄：**别怪我说话难听：这一战，绝不能让罗浮领衔。

**Jing Yuan:** Oh my, Merlin's Claw, are you afraid the Luofu might steal the Yaoqing's glory?

> **景元：**天击将军，莫不是怕罗浮摘了曜青战功？

**Feixiao:** This is no laughing matter. Between the Stellaron crisis and the Wardance ceremony... Though the chaos has settled, rumors and speculation are only growing by the day.

> **飞霄：**兹事体大，就别打趣了。星核之乱、演武仪典…乱象虽平，坊间流言蜚语可是有增无减。

**Feixiao:** With Elder Huaiyan around, those with ulterior motives won't be able to stir up any trouble. But once the Luofu gets their hands on the golden blood, it'll be a whole different story.

> **飞霄：**有炎老在，别有用心之徒掀不起风浪。可一旦罗浮夺来金血，事态就大为不同了。

**Feixiao:** With the kind of person Jingliu is, and her connection to you... I don't need to spell it out. There are countless ways this could be spun into something bigger.

> **飞霄：**镜流是何许人也，与你又有何渊源…不必我多说。借题发挥的法子，要多少有多少。

**Jing Yuan:** You're overthinking it, General Feixiao. When I volunteered for this task, I never expected unanimous support from the Alliance anyway.

> **景元：**飞霄将军多虑。我此番请缨，本就不求联盟内众口同声。

**Jing Yuan:** The Luofu will seize the golden blood and redeem itself. That'll be our first win. Then we'll leverage this golden blood in the negotiations, our second win. These two wins for the Luofu will make all three of us happy, and that'll be our third win.

> **景元：**罗浮斩获金血，戴罪立功，此为一胜；你我恃此金血，因便斡旋，此为二胜；罗浮二胜，我三人皆大欢喜，此为三胜——

**Jing Yuan:** Look, if this were a Sky-Faring Commission deal, we'd be rolling in money, wouldn't we?

> **景元：**瞧，这要是天舶司的买卖，不是赚得盆满钵满么？

**Yao Guang:** Maybe the Divine Foresight should change his name to the "Optimistic General" instead.

> **爻光：**我看「神策将军」改名叫「乐观将军」得了。

**Jing Yuan:** With the Deca-Light Reflection Barrier, the Yuque must understand even more than the Luofu how significant this battle is.

> **景元：**玉阙有「十方光映法界」傍身，定比罗浮更明白此战的意义。

**Jing Yuan:** Amphoreus's karma has vanished from the Matrix of Prescience. What omen does General Yao Guang see in this anomaly?

> **景元：**翁法罗斯的因果从穷观阵中消失了——在爻光将军看来，这一异象主何吉凶？

**Yao Guang:** In divination studies, we call this an "empty fortune." It involves the Aeons and is thus beyond what mere mortals can comprehend.

> **爻光：**卜筮学中，我们称之为「虚贞」：事涉星神，非凡人可窥全貌。

**Jing Yuan:** Your explanation isn't any clearer than Lady Fu's.

> **景元：**您这解释也不比符卿说得好懂。

**Yao Guang:** Come on, how hard is it to understand the word "uncertainty"?

> **爻光：**哎呀，「测不准」三个字，到底哪里不好懂了？

**Yao Guang:** For the Cobalt Head, the emergence of Irontomb is a calculated Instant, but I don't believe THEY will just sit around waiting for death.

> **爻光：**对于青金脑袋，「铁墓」出世是计算中的时刻，但我相信祂不会坐以待毙。

**Yao Guang:** And for the powers of the cosmos, this is their chance at survival and the key to turning the tide of interstellar affairs. The Astral Express is leading the formation of an allied force, but I can see each faction has its own hidden agenda.

> **爻光：**而对于银河势力，这是「一线生机」，也是扭转星际形势的关键。星穹列车牵头组建联军，但我看各方派系都暗藏小九九呢。

**Feixiao:** There are even divisions within the Alliance, so it's not that hard to imagine how the other factions will react.

> **飞霄：**联盟内部都有分歧，不难想象其余势力会如何。

**Feixiao:** Jing Yuan. I crossed paths with Celenova's Legion at the Battle of Sedounirama Star Cluster.

> **飞霄：**景元。赛杜尼拉默星群一战，我和星啸的军团交过手了。

**Feixiao:** Be extremely careful. The Ruin Legion's troops and weaponry are nothing to write home about. The IPC, even the Denizens of Abundance, could take them on. However, while Voidrangers are just expendable pawns of the Destruction, the real wild cards are—

> **飞霄：**务必小心。论军备、兵卒，烬灭军团不值一提，公司，甚至丰饶民都能与之一战。但虚卒不过是「毁灭」的耗材，真正的变数——

**Jing Yuan:** The "Lord Ravagers."

> **景元：**是「绝灭大君」。

**Feixiao:** Indeed. Nanook's Emanators are also THEIR weapons to burn down the Paths. I'm afraid conventional military tactics and strategies will be useless against them.

> **飞霄：**没错。纳努克的令使，也是祂燃烧命途的兵器。寻常的兵法、韬略，恐怕对他们不起作用。

**Feixiao:** My assessment is this: To completely take down a Ravager, we must disregard casualties and spare no cost. My only fear is...

> **飞霄：**这是我的判断——要彻底击落一名大君，必须不计伤亡，不惜代价，只怕……

**Feixiao:** One wrong move and the Luofu will fall into Phantylia's trap again, inching ever closer to Destruction.

> **飞霄：**只怕稍有不慎，罗浮又会落入幻胧的陷阱，离「毁灭」越来越近。

**Jing Yuan:** Doubt is exactly the demon she wants to plant in our hearts.

> **景元：**可「疑虑」二字，正是她意图在你我心中留下的心魔。

**Jing Yuan:** Do you still remember the beginning of the Alliance Oath? "To save future generations from war and bloody dispute."

> **景元：**还记得么？联盟誓言的开篇：「欲令后世免于侵凌攻伐、危疑苛暴之衅」。

**Jing Yuan:** The Reignbow's arrows have always aimed at any force that threatens the cosmos. Now that the Trailblaze walks alongside us, the Cloud Knights shall keep their oath as they have from the beginning...

> **景元：**帝弓的锋镝，从来指向一切威胁寰宇的灾祸。既然「开拓」道与我同，那云骑也当守誓如初……

**Jing Yuan:** I just hope those on the frontline will remain safe.

> **景元：**但愿战线最前方的他们，也能够平安哪。

### Enter Amphoreus and locate Irontomb from within the world

> *According to Herta's battle plan, the Express Crew will infiltrate deep into Amphoreus and coordinate with the Cosmic Alliance in a pincer attack to defeat Irontomb together.*

[Recap] Except for Pom-Pom, the entire Express Crew worked to locate the Irontomb from within Amphoreus. Meanwhile, Herta and Screwllum planned to attack the Scepter's core layer, the Demiurge Matrix, from the outside.

**Black Swan:** The Chronocognitive Anchor is sending a signal... Everyone, time to move out.

> **黑天鹅：**「识刻锚」传来了信号…各位，该动身了。

**Welt:** Did (Trailblazer) and the others make it?

> **瓦尔特：**开拓者他们，成功了吗？

**Herta:** Let me go over the plan one more time, just in case: You'll enter Amphoreus, reunite with your three separated companions, and try to locate Irontomb from within the world.

> **黑塔：**保险起见，我再重复一遍计划：你们要进入翁法罗斯，与失散的三兄妹汇合，设法从世界内部定位「铁墓」。

**Herta:** Who are you taking with you?

> **黑塔：**准备带谁？

**Welt:** The fate of the universe hangs in the balance. We can't afford any slip-ups. I suggest everyone, except Pom-Pom, join the fight.

> **瓦尔特：**这一战事关寰宇，容不得任何闪失。我建议——除帕姆外——全员出动。

**Black Swan:** We do need to be prepared for all possible contingencies. I have no objection to this.

> **黑天鹅：**需要应对一切可能的变数，我没有异议。

**Sunday:** I'll do whatever I can.

> **星期日：**愿尽绵薄之力。

**Herta:** Meanwhile, Screwy and I will continue attacking the Demiurge Matrix, that is, the Scepter's kernel, from the outside.

> **黑塔：**与此同时，我和螺丝会继续从外部攻击德谬歌矩阵，也就是权杖的内核层。

**Herta:** With our two-pronged attack, we'll turn this hunk of metal into scrap for sure. Now, we just wait for those two to get back.

> **黑塔：**咱们双管齐下，一定能让这台铁疙瘩彻底报废。现在——就等那俩人回来了。

**Herta:** Oh, and just like that, they're here!

> **黑塔：**喔，人这不就到齐了？

**Himeko:** Sorry to keep you waiting. With Planet Screwllum's help, the formation of the cosmic alliance to counter Irontomb is progressing smoothly.

> **姬子：**久等了。在螺丝星的帮助下，组建银河联军共御「铁墓」一事，推进得很顺利。

**Screwllum:** The cosmos has set its sights on Amphoreus, and now it's just waiting for... the first shot.

> **螺丝咕姆：**银河已将目光投向翁法罗斯，只待…一声枪响。

**Herta:** Well then, let's not waste any more time. Fourth Mirror!

> **黑塔：**那么，事不宜迟——第四面镜！

**Fourth Mirror:** It's my time to shine! Finally off the bench!

> **第四面镜：**轮到我登场啦！终于不用坐冷板凳了！

**Fourth Mirror:** Initiating Thought Refraction. Please read the safety protocols carefully...

> **第四面镜：**「思维折射」启动：请仔细阅读安全协议……

**Herta:** Huh?

> **黑塔：**嗯？

**Fourth Mirror:** Nah, just kidding! I've already checked the agreement box for everyone. And now, destination: Amphoreus!

> **第四面镜：**不，当然不用！我早就帮大家勾选同意啦——目标：翁法罗斯！

**Fourth Mirror:** Three, two, one... Launch!

> **第四面镜：**三，二，一…启动！

### Establish contact with the outside world and coordinate with the Cosmic Alliance

[Recap] The infinite cycle was destroyed. In the Vortex of Genesis, Himeko placed the Trailblaze beacon, attempting to establish contact with the outside world.

**Sunday:** ...The flow of memoria is so heavy here.

> **星期日：**…忆质的流向，如此沉重。

**Sunday:** Unlike Penacony's Dreamscape, this place has built up a true epic measured in millennia.

> **星期日：**与匹诺康尼的梦境截然不同，这里沉淀着真正以万年为计的史诗。

**Black Swan:** The Vortex of Genesis, where all myths begin and end. And in this new world created by (Trailblazer)...

> **黑天鹅：**创世涡心，一切神话的起点与终点。而在开拓者开创的新世界里……

**Black Swan:** The infinite cycles have been broken.

> **黑天鹅：**无尽的轮回已被打破了。

**Himeko:** Let's set up the Trailblaze beacon and establish contact with the outside world.

> **姬子：**布下「开拓」的信标，与外界建立联系吧。

**Black Swan:** ...I'll leave it to you then. It'd be better for me to not show myself in front of the Garden of Recollection.

> **黑天鹅：**那…交给各位了。我不便在流光忆庭面前现身。

**Black Swan:** And Mr. Sunday, you probably shouldn't show yourself either.

> **黑天鹅：**还有星期日先生，也不适合露面，对吧？

**Sunday:** Agreed.

> **星期日：**同意。

[Recap] The Galaxy Rangers and the Family did not answer. The Xianzhou Alliance and the Interastral Peace Corporation replied to the Express Crew's message and, at the alliance meeting, engaged in intense negotiations regarding cooperation.

**Himeko:** The Astral Express has landed. If you receive this message, please respond immediately. Over.

> **姬子：**星穹列车已着陆。若能接收到这条讯息，请立即回复。完毕。

**Fu Xuan:** This is the Xianzhou Alliance, communications normal.

> **符玄：**这里是仙舟联盟，通讯正常。

**Aventurine:** Interastral Peace Corporation, communications normal.

> **砂金：**星际和平公司，通讯正常。

**Aventurine:** No response from the Galaxy Rangers? Not surprising. But I didn't expect the Family to also stay silent at such a critical moment.

> **砂金：**巡海游侠没有回应？倒也正常。只是没想到，这紧要关头家族却默不作声。

**Jade:** That's a pretty blatant disregard from the Family for Penacony's independent director, I'd say. Long time no see, Miss Himeko.

> **翡翠：**未免太不把匹诺康尼的独立董事放在眼里了。好久不见，姬子小姐。

**Himeko:** It's been quite a while, Madam Jade.

> **姬子：**久违了，翡翠女士。

**Jade:** The significance of this battle rivals that of the campaign against Zulo. Only through victory can we disperse the shadow of Irontomb that looms over countless worlds.

> **翡翠：**这场战役的重要性，足以和「诛罗讨伐战」相提并论。唯有得胜，方能驱散笼罩万千世界的「铁墓」阴影。

**Jade:** The Ten Stonehearts shall spare no effort for such a grand mission for the Preservation.

> **翡翠：**如此「存护」大业，石心十人必当倾尽全力。

**Aventurine:** Madam Jade led the Intelligentsia Guild in conducting specialized research on Irontomb's past activities.

> **砂金：**翡翠女士牵头博识学会，对铁墓的过往活动进行了专项研究。

**Aventurine:** The bad news is, the collapse of the Erudition won't just affect inorganic beings.

> **砂金：**一个坏消息，「智识」的溃败不限于无机体。

**Welt:** You mean Irontomb can infect organic life too?

> **瓦尔特：**你的意思是，铁墓还会感染有机生命？

**Aventurine:** The good new is, so far, its effects on organic life are under control. The infection progresses slowly, and the resulting neuropathy is non-fatal.

> **砂金：**好消息是：截至目前，它对有机体的影响可控。感染进程缓慢，造成的神经病变也不致命。

**Aventurine:** In any case, the IPC can guarantee targeted vaccine coverage within 48 system hours in all star zones within the medical system.

> **砂金：**无论如何，公司会在48个系统时内，确保医疗体系内的星域实现针对性疫苗覆盖。

**Fu Xuan:** What about other star zones? At a time like this, are the followers of the Firmamend Arbiter still trying to make a profit?

> **符玄：**那其他星域呢？都这时候了，补天司命的拥趸还打着生意算盘？

**Jade:** I remember you, Master Diviner. Your performance during the Third Denizens of Abundance War was quite impressive.

> **翡翠：**我记得您，太卜。第三次丰饶民战争，您的表现令人印象深刻。

**Jade:** Please rest assured, all worlds can purchase vaccines from the IPC with a humanitarian discount. That's the current policy, at the very least.

> **翡翠：**请放心，所有世界都可以向公司购买疫苗，并享受人道主义折扣——目前的方针是这样。

**Jade:** But if the Xianzhou Alliance is willing to assist with the IPC's assets in Epsilon and other star systems, we'll have more cards to play when reiterating to the board the necessity of deep cooperation.

> **翡翠：**但如果仙舟联盟愿意驰援公司在艾普瑟隆等星系的资产，我们就有更多手牌，向董事会重申深度合作的必要性。

**Fu Xuan:** It makes sense, since Interastral Peace Entertainment's headquarters is in Epsilon. But why are all the other worlds on the list...

> **符玄：**星际和平娱乐的总部在艾普瑟隆，倒是不难理解。但名单上的其他世界……

**Fu Xuan:** Places that the Elation has visited?

> **符玄：**为何尽是些「欢愉」垂迹之地？

**Aventurine:** Think of it as a long-term investment. Please understand that everyone at Pier Point is swamped with Irontomb work, especially the Marketing Development Department. At this rate, even the Ten Stonehearts can only do so much.

> **砂金：**就把它当作一场长远的投资吧。还请见谅：庇尔波因特上下都在为铁墓忙作一团——尤其是市场开拓部——石心十人难免力有不逮。

**Aventurine:** Having some assistance from the alliance would surely benefit the entire cosmos, wouldn't you agree?

> **砂金：**若有联盟相助，对全银河都有益无害，不是么？

**Fu Xuan:** As an old Xianzhou saying goes: Those who succeed should help all under heaven. Your proposal must be decided on by the Seven Arbiter-Generals. I'm just here to relay the message.

> **符玄：**仙舟古语有云，达则兼济天下。你们的提议自有七天将裁夺，我只负责转达。

**Fu Xuan:** In times of crisis, the Luofu will not hesitate to help those in need. However, the prerequisite is that the IPC must unconditionally assist the Alliance with emergency rescue operations in the disaster area.

> **符玄：**大难当前，若能扶危济困，罗浮定义不容辞。但一切的前提是，公司必须无条件协助联盟在灾区的抢险救援工作。

**Jade:** Of course. In the name of Preservation, all for the Amber Lord.

> **翡翠：**当然——以「存护」的名义，一切献给琥珀王。

**Himeko:** Thank you all for your selfless dedication.

> **姬子：**感谢各位无私奉献。

[Recap] With the fate of the cosmos at stake, every faction had their own interests to consider. Either way, wait for some to good news. Welt suggested delving into the Vortex of Genesis to examine the interior of Amphoreus.

**Black Swan:** It's clear that each party still has qualms about everything.

> **黑天鹅：**看得出来，各方都有些投鼠忌器。

**Sunday:** The clash between Destruction and Erudition isn't just about the fate of the cosmos. It's a moment for all factions to reshuffle their cards.

> **星期日：**「毁灭」与「智识」的交锋，不仅关乎银河存亡，更是各方势力洗牌的时刻啊。

**Himeko:** You can never be too careful when battling a Lord Ravager. All are equal in matters of life and death.

> **姬子：**与一位绝灭大君为敌，再谨慎都不为过。存亡面前，人人平等。

**Welt:** In any case, we can only hope for the best in all that's to come.

**Welt:** It's about time we dive into the Vortex and see what Amphoreus looks like on the inside.

> **瓦尔特：**无论如何，静候佳音吧。

*(After previous dialogue)*

**Welt:** I wonder where (Trailblazer) and the others are right now...

> **瓦尔特：**我们也是时候深入涡心，去看看翁法罗斯内部的样貌了。

### (Optional) Try to contact allies

> *The campaign against Irontomb decides the fate of the cosmos, and each faction weighs its own interests. After the alliance meeting, perhaps it is time to deepen ties with allies, to gather strength from all sides.*

*(After interacting with a Chronocognitive Anchor, it will disappear after exiting dialogue)*

[Recap] Himeko attempted to contact the IPC representative. Aventurine explained that the Strategic Investment Department was hamstrung and powerless regarding the Irontomb situation. In these trying times, Aventurine hoped the Astral Express would remember the sacrifices made by the Ten Stonehearts.

*(Contact the IPC)*

- ▸ Investigate

You activate the Chronocognitive Anchor, attempting to contact the IPC representative...

**Aventurine:** I regret to inform you, Miss Himeko, that even the Strategic Investment Department is constrained when it comes to the Irontomb issue. And it would be difficult to meet the expectations of the Astral Express.

**Aventurine:** However, Madam Jade instructed us to look after our Nameless friends as much as possible. So let me offer you all a small suggestion, just from my personal perspective.

**Aventurine:** Other departments at the IPC might also take this opportunity to extend an olive branch to you...

**Aventurine:** When that time comes, I hope the Astral Express will remember that the Ten Stonehearts were willing to stand with the Trailblaze even though we were in a difficult position, and more importantly, we were the only ones willing to do so.

**Aventurine:** Sounds like a threat, doesn't it? But trust me, every word comes straight from the heart.

**Himeko:** Has the internal conflict within the IPC really gotten this bad?

**Aventurine:** Everyone knows about the bad blood between Diamond and Oswaldo, but at least until now they've had an uneasy truce...

**Aventurine:** Until the Lord Ravager Archforger caught the Board's attention... I'll leave it at that, as it's confidential information. Hope you can understand.

**Himeko:** I understand. Our immediate priority is to address the Irontomb crisis. Let's hope for success from the start. This might help ease everyone's pressing concerns.

[Recap] Himeko attempted to contact the Xianzhou Alliance representative. According to Fu Xuan, this military deployment was personally decided by the Marshal, and the Irontomb battle did offer something that Xianzhou sought. As for the jade abacus of allying oath, it can be saved for future use.

*(Contact the Xianzhou Alliance)*

- ▸ Investigate

You activate the Chronocognitive Anchor, attempting to contact the Xianzhou Alliance representative...

**Fu Xuan:** Good news, Miss Himeko. The Seven Arbiter-Generals have reconsidered the current situation, and I'm here to relay their message. Officially, the Alliance's combat forces will still be limited to the Luofu, but the Yaoqing has also been deployed in the surrounding war zone as a contingency.

**Himeko:** Words cannot express our gratitude. Getting the Hunt to agree on sending troops must have taken quite some effort from General Jing Yuan.

**Fu Xuan:** The Luofu already considers the Express an important ally. We also have no intention of hiding the fact that there is indeed something the Xianzhou seeks from this Irontomb operation.

**Fu Xuan:** But this time, the Marshal herself decided to send out troops. With a formidable enemy before us, the Alliance has no reason to back down, nor will we be content to take a back seat.

**Fu Xuan:** If we can stand together with civilizations across the universe to overcome this crisis, then we must do so, even at the cost of our own lives.

**Himeko:** The Alliance truly embodies the principle of righteousness. It's fortunate that the cosmos has great heroes like you.

**Fu Xuan:** You're too kind. As for that jade abacus of allying oath, you can save it for when you're in more dire need.

[Recap] Himeko attempted to contact the Family's representative. Robin reported that the Family's response to the Irontomb battle has been minimal. However, in her personal capacity, Robin would lead the Tuners and the Charity Foundation to the frontlines.

*(Contact the Family)*

- ▸ Investigate

You activate the Chronocognitive Anchor, attempting to contact The Family representative...

**Robin:** ...Miss Himeko, is that you?

**Himeko:** Yes. And you are... Miss Robin?

**Robin:** ...Yes. I apologize for not being able to respond during the meeting earlier, and even now I can only appear in disguise.

**Robin:** On behalf of the Penaconian Family branch, I extend my sincerest apologies to everyone. We tried to request aid from The Family, but we essentially received no response.

**Himeko:** I've heard about the internal conflicts within The Family. Miss Robin, there's no need to apologize. Every bit of strength is precious at this moment.

**Robin:** Thank you for understanding. The good news is, since you're one of the shareholders of Penacony, the family heads have agreed to allow me to lead all Asdanian tuners to the frontlines.

**Robin:** My charitable foundation will also do as much as possible to provide support in this battle. They'll ensure that proper arrangements are provided for those wounded on the frontlines and for residents in affected areas.

**Himeko:** Miss Robin, I'm beyond moved by your spirit of self-sacrifice. The Astral Express would also like to make a promise that we won't leave any companion behind.

[Recap] Himeko attempted to contact the Galaxy Ranger representative, but received no response. It was expected, given that the Rangers had always been an elusive bunch.

*(Contact the Galaxy Rangers)*

- ▸ Investigate

You activate the Chronocognitive Anchor, attempting to contact the Galaxy Ranger representative...

...

No response from the Chronocognitive Anchor.

**Himeko:** (Of course, we would never know the Rangers' comings and goings.)

[Recap] Himeko responded to the Chronocognitive Anchor's contact request. Bronya reported that Belobog had received Topaz's support and was ready to handle the disaster.

*(Contact Belobog)*

- ▸ Investigate

The Chronocognitive Anchor receives a contact request, you try to respond...

**Bronya:** Hello...? How's the signal? Can you hear us?

> **布洛妮娅：**喂喂…信号怎么样？能听到我们说话吗？

**Serval:** We're through! I can see the video feed. Everything's working perfectly!

> **希露瓦：**通了，通了！能看到影像了，肯定没问题。

**Himeko:** Miss Bronya, Miss Serval? What a surprise! It's been a while.

> **姬子：**布洛妮娅小姐，希露瓦小姐？真是意外，久违了。

**Bronya:** We've already heard from Miss Topaz about the Irontomb operation. She made a special trip to deliver the vaccine to Belobog.

> **布洛妮娅：**铁墓一役，我们已经听托帕小姐说了。她亲自跑了一趟，为贝洛伯格送来了疫苗。

**Bronya:** I can't believe the Express Crew is at the eye of that storm... Is everything going smoothly?

> **布洛妮娅：**没想到列车组处在这场风暴的中心…你们还顺利吗？

**Himeko:** Don't worry, we're still following the plan step by step. How's the situation in Belobog?

> **姬子：**放心，我们还在按部就班地推行计划。贝洛伯格那边如何？

**Topaz:** Jarilo-VI is located on the outskirts of the Preservation barricade. While it won't bear the brunt of the first wave, there's still a risk from the aftershocks.

> **托帕：**雅利洛-Ⅵ位于「存护」壁垒外沿。虽然不会承受第一波冲击，但仍有余波威胁风险。

**Serval:** That kid Gepard and Pela are busy organizing the Silvermane Guards, having them consolidate their lines and dig trenches. Seele and Lynx just got back from the Geomarrow Vein production chain... Hey, do you want to say something to them?

> **希露瓦：**杰帕德那小子和佩拉一起，忙着组织银鬃铁卫收缩战线、修筑战壕呢；希儿和玲可刚从地髓矿脉的生产链回来…哎，要不要说两句？

**Seele:** ...That would be unnecessary. It'll only shake their morale. Just tell them to stay safe.

> **希儿：**…没什么好说的，动摇军心。叫他们注意安全就行。

**Himeko:** Miss Bronya, I'm afraid this disaster will be far more terrible than you can imagine. Please be sure to follow Miss Topaz's instructions and make thorough preparations.

> **姬子：**布洛妮娅小姐，这场灾难恐怕会比你们想象中更加可怕，请务必听从托帕小姐指示，做好万全准备。

**Bronya:** Of course. We have resealed most of the Engine of Creation beneath the ice to prevent the will of the Preservation from becoming an accomplice to the Destruction.

> **布洛妮娅：**当然。我们已经将绝大部分造物引擎重新封入冰下，以防「存护」之志成为「毁灭」的帮凶。

**Topaz:** Don't worry, Miss Himeko. Belobog is far more resilient than we imagined.

> **托帕：**放心，姬子小姐，贝洛伯格远比我们想象中更坚韧。

**Serval:** We really wish we could help... but Jarilo-VI has only just woken up from its hibernation.

> **希露瓦：**我们真的很希望能帮上忙…可惜，雅利洛-Ⅵ也只是刚从冬眠中苏醒的冰原熊而已。

**Serval:** The Astral Express, standing at the forefront of disaster, is the hope for many ordinary civilizations across the universe.

> **希露瓦：**站在灾难最前方的星穹列车，才是众多宇宙平凡文明的希望哪。

**Bronya:** In the face of a massive catastrophe like this one, relatively smaller civilizations... have never had many options.

> **布洛妮娅：**在如此宏观的灾难面前，相对弱小的文明…一向没有更多选择。

**Seele:** ...

> **希儿：**……

**Bronya:** But that's exactly why we have a resolve that's second to none.

> **布洛妮娅：**但也正因如此，我们才有不输给任何人的决心。

**Himeko:** If anything goes wrong... Miss Topaz, I hope you can help out and rescue the affected citizens.

> **姬子：**如有不测…还请托帕小姐能够帮忙，救援受灾民众。

**Topaz:** No problem. Also, didn't the Xianzhou sign a friendship agreement with Jarilo-VI earlier? They won't stand by idly either.

> **托帕：**没问题。先前仙舟不是和雅利洛-VI签订了友好协议吗？他们也不会袖手旁观。

**Bronya:** ...

> **布洛妮娅：**……

**Bronya:** I appreciate your kindness, everyone. But Jarilo-VI is our home. We will defend it to our last breath, until the last Belobogian falls.

> **布洛妮娅：**各位的好意，我心领了。但雅利洛-VI是我们的家。我们会誓死坚守，直到最后一个贝洛伯格人倒下。

**Himeko:** Miss Bronya...

### Follow the call of the prophecy to Dawncloud

> *The isolation of Amphoreus has been shattered, and the three Nameless forerunners have become Titans of the new world. The holy city's residents pray to the Worldbearing (Trailblazer), devoutly awaiting the divine prophecy to descend upon the world.*

[Recap] Residents of the holy city all gathered at Dawncloud to witness your awakening. March 7th beckoned the Express Crew to come closer and see how the prophecy of "Worldbearing" would change this world.

## 160　The Witch's Ardent Research　魔女的孤诣课题

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Nightmare's Echo" Great Tomb of the Nameless Titan　|　角色：Lygus; The Herta; Screwllum; Trailblazer; Cyrene; Phainon; Aglaea; Dan Heng; Mem; Irontomb; Ruan Mei; Acha; Polka Kakamond; Dr. Primitive; Nous　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Traverse Stars and Stride Cosmos'' completed

### Explore the Matrix, seek the secrets of the Demiurge

> *The Scepter's kernel is severely damaged, little more than rubble. Seeking traces of the Demiurge here would be like finding a needle in a haystack. How revealing... This only confirms one thing... I'm exactly where I need to be.*

**Herta:** Is it just me, or does this Demiurge Matrix...

> **黑塔：**话虽如此，是我的错觉吗？这德谬歌矩阵……

**Herta:** Feel completely dead? It's nothing like how the Memokeeper described it.

> **黑塔：**怎么死气沉沉的，跟那忆者的形容天差地别。

**Screwllum:** The environmental readings are all wrong. Conclusion: this must be what the "kernel" actually looks like in the real world.

> **螺丝咕姆：**环境参数严重不符。结论：这是「内核层」在现实中的样子。

**Herta:** What did Zandar do to the Scepter...?

> **黑塔：**「赞达尔」那家伙，到底对权杖做了什么？

**Screwllum:** ...

> **螺丝咕姆：**……

**Screwllum:** "At the end of light, there must be silence. Certain, pure, and absolute darkness."

> **螺丝咕姆：**「光线的终点，必须是一团寂静、确凿、纯粹的黑暗」。

**Herta:** Not the time to quote the Lord of Silence. Bad omen. Either way, we have to press on...

> **黑塔：**这会儿就别引用「寂静领主」的名言了，不吉利。无论如何，我们都得继续深入……

**Herta:** Until we uncover the truth behind the 13th Titan.

> **黑塔：**直到揪出第十三位泰坦的秘密。

**Screwllum:** The buried past is the key to stopping Irontomb. That is, if it even exists.

> **螺丝咕姆：**被掩埋的过往，正是对抗「铁墓」的关键。但前提是，它真的存在。

**Herta:** Zandar says the Demiurge never existed. Who actually buys that? You can erase the records, but not the truth.

> **黑塔：**「赞达尔」说德谬歌从未诞生——谁信？记录可以被抹除，但真相不能。

**Herta:** It vanished too cleanly. No traces, like someone yelling, "Look! There's nothing here!"

> **黑塔：**它消失得太彻底了，一丝痕迹都没留下，就好像有人刻意大喊——「这里什么都没有。」

**Herta:** Too bad I'm the type who can't resist digging deeper. Looks like I'll be counting on you again, Screwy. Let's tear this system inside out.

> **黑塔：**可惜，本人最爱刨根问底。这次又得仰仗你了，螺丝，一起把系统翻个底朝天吧。

**Screwllum:** Always happy to help. But as your academic partner, I have to ask—

> **螺丝咕姆：**乐意效劳。但作为学术伙伴，我必须指出——

**Screwllum:** Herta, what if we fail?

> **螺丝咕姆：**黑塔，如果我们失败了呢？

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Screwllum:** Let's say the Demiurge really doesn't exist. Do you have a backup plan?

> **螺丝咕姆：**假设「德谬歌」并不存在，你是否准备了预案？

**Herta:** Backup plan? Of course. If we can't find the key...

> **黑塔：**备用方案？当然有。如果实在找不着钥匙……

**Herta:** We turn the "lock" into the key.

> **黑塔：**就让「锁」来当钥匙吧。

**Screwllum:** That plan's cost... is incalculable. It shouldn't even be on the table.

> **螺丝咕姆：**该方案的损耗…无法计算。它不该被列为选项。

**Herta:** Then let's hope we never have to use it.

> **黑塔：**那就祈祷我们用不上吧。

**Herta:** There's no point worrying about the end just yet. One step at a time. Let's go. We don't want to leave the others waiting.

> **黑塔：**别聊那么远的话题，凡事都得一步一步来。走了，别让外面的人等太久。

### Board the Mistletoe

> *Screwllum's treasure looks promising. Let's hope it works just as well on the Matrix.*

*(Approach the "Mistletoe")*

> **黑塔：**你的花园怎么都带点毒？

**Screwllum:** Also a personal preference.

> **螺丝咕姆：**同样是个人爱好。

> **黑塔：**它让我想起上一回，咱们跟那朋克洛德小鬼交手的时候。她管这种渗透工具叫什么来着…喔，脏数据壳。用来包裹住核心，骗过最顶级的防火墙。

**Herta:** But yours is way too clean. Not even a hint of real-world grit.

> **黑塔：**不过，你这个太干净了，一点烟火气都没有。

**Screwllum:** Herta, your tone is unusually emotional.

> **螺丝咕姆：**黑塔，你的语气有非典型波动。

**Screwllum:** Suggestion: Let's get back to the topic at hand.

> **螺丝咕姆：**提议：我们应当继续刚才的话题。

> **黑塔：**我以为那个话题早过去了。

**Screwllum:** Unfortunately, current conditions won't let me ignore any variables. Especially the most important one: your will.

> **螺丝咕姆：**现状不允许我忽视任何变量，尤其是最关键的那一道：你的意志。

**Screwllum:** Irontomb never stops, it is evolving faster than any of our worst projections.

> **螺丝咕姆：**「铁墓」的自我迭代不会停止，其进化速度已超过最坏预期。

**Screwllum:** Every Instant in the Aeon's calculation is like a sword hanging over the cosmos. Every move humanity makes could speed up its arrival.

> **螺丝咕姆：**星神计算中的时刻，就像一柄悬在银河头顶的利剑。人们做出的每一步抉择，都可能加速它的到来。

**Herta:** Which is exactly why we can't mess this up.

> **黑塔：**所以，我们更不能失手。

**Herta:** And now Droidhead wants to hand down judgment to the poor cosmos again: Borderstar Trade War, Emperor's War, Rubert's death... every time THEIR Instant arrived, it meant another bloodbath for humanity.

> **黑塔：**机器头又要给可怜的银河下判决了：边星贸易战争、帝皇战争、鲁珀特之死，每一次祂的时刻应验，都意味着人类要经历一场血洗。

**Screwllum:** But in this ultimate battle of Erudition, you and I are supposed to be players, not pawns.

> **螺丝咕姆：**但在「智识」的终极博弈里，你我应当成为棋手，而不是棋子。

**Screwllum:** If you choose to burn yourself out as a "resource," then everything we've worked for means nothing.

> **螺丝咕姆：**一旦你选择将自己用作「耗材」，那我们所做的一切，将失去意义。

**Screwllum:** Herta, the cosmos won't just stand by and let a genius throw their life away...

> **螺丝咕姆：**黑塔，你该明白：银河不会坐视一位天才牺牲自己——

**Screwllum:** And neither will I.

> **螺丝咕姆：**我亦不例外。

**Herta:** No need to kill the mood. This is why we're here, isn't it? We need to find the Demiurge and that lost key.

> **黑塔：**别说这种扫兴话。所以我们才要过来，不是么？找到「德谬歌」，那把丢失的钥匙。

**Herta:** I'm not here to be a "Deliverer." I just want to beat Zandar and Droidhead.

> **黑塔：**我可没兴趣当「救世主」。我只想赢——赢过「赞达尔」，还有机器头。

**Herta:** Right now, I just need to know: are you with me?

> **黑塔：**现在——我只关心，你愿意站在我这边么？

**Screwllum:** ...

> **螺丝咕姆：**……

**Screwllum:** Of course. Time to board. After you.

> **螺丝咕姆：**当然。该登机了，你先请。

**Herta:** Let's tidy this place up and see what this "Demiurge" really is.

> **黑塔：**让我们把这地方收拾干净，看看「德谬歌」到底是何方神圣。

> **黑塔：**所谓的「亲手扼杀」，原来是这么回事啊。

> **黑塔：**名为「德谬歌」的生命形态，正是权杖原本的演算目标。

**Screwllum:** "The prime mover of life"... that's what Scepter δ-me13 was originally tasked with. Even after it got scrapped, the query kept running. Yet, the true value lies in the process of solving it, not the answer itself...

> **螺丝咕姆：**「生命的第一因」——这是权杖δ-me13最初负责的课题，遭到废弃后，其求解仍在进行。虽然这一问题的价值在于求解过程，而非答案本身……

**Screwllum:** Then... does this mean finding the Demiurge is hopeless?

> **螺丝咕姆：**这是否意味着，找到德谬歌的希望已经不复存在？

> **黑塔：**不，在我看来，恰恰相反。

> **黑塔：**这代表「赞达尔」束手无策。他无法掌控德谬歌，就像他无法掌控机器头，只能用尽一切手段抹去它的存在。

**Herta:** But he failed. It still left a mark.

> **黑塔：**可惜，它还是留下了痕迹。

> **黑塔：**我们必须假设，这两件事同时成立。如此一来，「矛盾」就会指向——

> **螺丝咕姆：**真相。

## 161　Hero, Return to Dawn in Mortality　英雄啊，归以凡身向侵晨

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域："Memortis Shore" Ruins of Time　|　角色：Trailblazer; March 7th; Dan Heng • Permansor Terrae; Cyrene; Mydei; Cipher; Phainon; Castorice; Hyacine; Little Ica; Anaxa; Hysilens; Cerydra; Lygus; Aglaea; Tribbie; Trianne; Trinnon; The Herta; Irontomb; Nous; Audata; Hieronymus; Himeko; Black Swan; Screwllum; Welt; Sunday; Asta; Ruan Mei　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''The Witch's Ardent Research'' completed

### Enter the Ruins of Time and begin the final "Flame-Chase Journey"

> *The Ruins of Time... This is the kernel of the Scepter. Here stands Phainon, who forged a prison with rage to contain the hatred of Irontomb. If we wish to face the Lord Ravager head-on, we must embark on our final Flame-Chase Journey, gather the destinies of the Chrysos Heirs who have ascended into Titans, and reach Phainon with their aid to dissolve the Blazing Sun's obsession.*

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** Never thought I'd one day be riding a train that's literally the incarnation of (Trailblazer)...

> **永夜之帷，三月七：**真没想到，咱有朝一日还能坐上开拓者化身的列车……

**Cyrene:** I wonder what the real Astral Express feels like. Hopefully not this... bumpy.

> **昔涟：**不知真正的星穹列车坐感如何，但愿不会这么…颠簸。

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** Finally... we're here.

> **磐岩之脊，丹恒：**总算…到站了。

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** Dust and sand in the air, and crumbling ruins all around.

> **磐岩之脊，丹恒：**漫天风沙，还有破碎的废墟。

**Veil of Evernight, March 7th:** This heat is brutal! Not even the Trailblaze can take it...

> **永夜之帷，三月七：**好可怕的热浪，连「开拓」都抵御不了……

**Pillar of Stone, Dan Heng:** You could say this prison was forged from Phainon's fury.

> **磐岩之脊，丹恒：**这座监牢，说是以白厄的怒火铸成也不为过。

**Cyrene:** But fury never lasts forever...

> **昔涟：**但愤怒总有平息的那一天……

**Cyrene:** That's why we're here, right, (Trailblazer)?

> **昔涟：**我们正是为此而来，对吧，开拓者？

- ▸ Time to go. Let's not keep Phainon waiting.
- ▸ Together, we'll all become Deliverers!
- ▸ Now, the entire cosmos stands with the Trailblaze.

**Throne of Worlds, (Trailblazer):** Let's embark on the final Flame-Chase Journey.

> **全世之座，开拓者：**启程吧——踏上最后的「逐火之旅」。

### Gather the "Last Titans" and prepare to face Irontomb

*(Approach the door)*

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** So this is what lies at the end of the west wind... a ravaged battlefield.

*(Approach Mydei)*

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** ...

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** (Trailblazer) of the Astral Express, right?

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**星穹列车的开拓者，对么？

**Cyrene:** Looks like I don't need to explain anything.

> **昔涟：**看来，不用再多做解释了呀。

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** I am the God of Strife, Mydei of Castrum Kremnos. In this body rests thirty million lifetimes of cause and effect.

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**我是「纷争」的神王，也是悬锋城的「万敌」。三千万世因果加诸此身——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** We, the last of the Twelve Titans, stand ready to fight for the world's destiny.

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** Indeed. That journey remains vivid in my memory. I will not forget it.

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**最初的涟漪，她理应和所有人一起抵达故事的结局。

> **磐岩之脊，丹恒：**我听见了…白厄的心跳。很微弱，恐怕他只剩一息尚存。

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**三千万次轮回，我与「侵晨」交锋过不可数的日夜。若你了解那个男人，就会知道……

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** It is exactly that last flicker of flame that will never go out.

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**这一世，「纷争」的诗篇将不再以据守悬锋作结，而要踏上不绝的征程——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** I am Mydeimos, the Lance of Fury. I trade a thousand scars and a hundred lives of mine...

> **天谴之矛，迈德漠斯：**我乃天谴之矛，迈德漠斯。以我的一千道伤疤和一百条性命——

**Mydeimos, Lance of Fury:** For the Destruction's eternal end!

Wave 2: Tide-Eroded Blade ×2, Cyclonic Swarm Mother ×1

### Talk to Hysilens and Anaxa

[Recap] Anaxa and Hysilens discussed banquets and blasphemy. Anaxa believed blasphemy was a phenomenon rather than a goal. Geniuses share lethal truths, while madmen try to force them upon others.

**Anaxa:** Based on my calculations, there are countless celestial bodies with water in the cosmos.

> **那刻夏：**依我推断，这银河中存在水体的天体不计其数。

**Anaxa:** But I'm afraid not all "oceans" are suitable for life to inhabit. You must take this into consideration as well.

**Hysilens:** As expected of a professor from the Grove... To be so certain even about things you've never seen with your own eyes.

> **海瑟音：**不愧是树庭的教授…即便对于从未亲眼见过的事物，竟也能如此笃定。

> **那刻夏：**我当你是在褒扬我了。

*(Talk to Anaxa)*

**Anaxa:** By the way, I heard that the Imperator's Flame-Chase Army liked to hold feasts at the Luminary Throne?

**Hysilens:** That's true. However, sometimes Dux Carminum and I feel it's a bit... inappropriate to drink so freely before the throne of the most revered Titan in the Grove...

> **海瑟音：**此事不假。不过，我和吟风爵偶尔也会觉得，在树庭最敬重的泰坦座前恣意畅饮，似有不妥……

**Anaxa:** Inappropriate? What's inappropriate about it? I only wish I were born earlier. I would've partied like that from the Month of Gate all the way to the Month of Fortune!

> **那刻夏：**不妥？何来不妥！我只恨自己晚生了许多年，不然那样的宴会，我巴不得从门关月开到机缘月！

**Hysilens:** Uh...

> **海瑟音：**这……

- ▸ Now this one's a heavyweight blasphemer.

> ▸ 这位更是重量级的渎神者。

- ▸ He must have a lot in common with Terravox.

> ▸ 他一定和荒笛很有共同语言。

**Anaxa:** Blasphemy is just a phenomenon, not the end goal. I'm sure there are plenty of people across the stars who question these so-called "Aeons" or "Paths."

> **那刻夏：**渎神只是现象，不是目的。放眼这星间，想必对所谓「星神」或「命途」心存质疑者亦不在少数。

**Anaxa:** Didn't I spend some time in that Lycurgus's head? Tsk tsk. I sure learned quite a bit about "blasphemy" from that experience.

> **那刻夏：**我不是在那吕枯耳戈斯的脑袋里待了些时日么？啧啧，可是让我学到不少「渎神」的知识啊。

**Anaxa:** Humans create gods, and gods shape humans. Who's really the one trapped? The creator, or the believer?

> **那刻夏：**人创造神，神塑造人。造物主与信徒，究竟谁才是受困的一方呢？

**Hysilens:** ...Professor Anaxagoras, let me just check one thing... You haven't bought into those twisted theories of that Theoros, have you?

> **海瑟音：**…阿那克萨戈拉斯教授，我姑且确认一句，你该不会理解了那神礼观众的歪理吧？

**Anaxa:** Don't worry. The difference between a genius and a madman is that a genius shares lethal truths, while a madman tries to force them upon others.

> **那刻夏：**不必担忧。天才与疯子的差别，就在于天才会将致命的真理共享，而疯子却试图将其强加于他人。

**Hysilens:** ...I see. It's like drinking honey brew at a banquet. Likeable folks invite others to raise their cups together, while the unlikeable ones force others to drink with them.

> **海瑟音：**…我明白了，大概就像宴会上喝蜜酿的时候，讨喜的人会举盏相邀，但不讨喜的人会碰杯强求。

**Anaxa:** Being able to break down complex information into common sense like that... You're quite the scholar yourself.

> **那刻夏：**能够将复杂的语料简化为常识，你也像一名合格的学者了。

> ▸ 她在哪…？

> **白厄：**星穹列车…实话说，很有「家园」的感觉。

**Phainon:** I can imagine how much you, Dan Heng, and Miss March must have missed everything here when you were trapped in Amphoreus. The beautiful windows, the soothing music, your fellow travelers on the Express...

> **白厄：**我能想象，你、丹恒、还有三月小姐，你们被困在翁法罗斯时，一定十分想念这里的一切：漂亮的窗户，惬意的音乐，列车上的旅伴……

**Phainon:** Like how golden wheat stalks sway in my dreams every night through the countless cycles...

> **白厄：**就像无数次轮回中的每个夜晚，金色的麦穗总会在我的梦中摇曳。

- ▸ Let's go on a journey together, partner.

> ▸ 一起去旅行吧，搭档。

> **白厄：**仙舟联盟，匹诺康尼…都怪我的想象力太匮乏，光听丹恒的描述，我完全想象不出那些世界的样貌。

**Cyrene:** Thank you, Phainon.

[Recap] The fall of Irontomb heralded the decline of the Scepter. Cyrene preserved the memories of Amphoreus in As I've Written and asked you to safeguard it well. You now stood at the epilogue of As I've Written.

**Cyrene:** After such a good sleep, you must be bursting with energy now, right, (Trailblazer)?

> **昔涟：**好好地睡了一觉，现在一定活力满满了吧，开拓者？

> **昔涟：**不好意思啦。第一次来列车作客，本来想请各位都守规矩点的…但大家就是一个个的个性十足呢。你懂的，对吧？

> ▸ 这一刻…是真实的吗？

> **昔涟：**我们合力战胜了铁墓；它试图化作万亿簇病毒，侵染银河；但英雄们以自身投入金焰，成为箭矢，净化了「毁灭」……

**Cyrene:** The fall of Irontomb also symbolizes the demise of Scepter δ-me13. Amphoreus — the home we sacrificed everything for — now exists only in the memories of the cosmos.

> **昔涟：**而铁墓的殒落，也象征着权杖δ-me13的衰亡。翁法罗斯——我们为之付出一切的家园——如今只存在于银河的记忆中了。

- ▸ So... this is just an illusion.

> ▸ 所以…这只是一片幻境。

> **开拓者：**只是一场梦…一片幻境。

> **昔涟：**嘻……

**(Trailblazer):** ...?

> **昔涟：**在你以「开拓」冲破时空，背负全世「记忆」回到现实的一霎——

**Cyrene:** That world, once shackled by "data," became a seed waiting to sprout in the cosmos.

**Cyrene:** Perhaps we, too, have already left long trails on our respective paths.

> **昔涟：**说不定，我们也在各自的命途上留下了长长的足迹呢。

- ▸ You are all free.

> ▸ 你们自由了。

> **昔涟：**自由，多美妙的词语呀。

**Cyrene:** But that alone isn't enough to make everyone a true "life form" yet.

> **昔涟：**不过，这还不足以让大家成为真正的「生命」哦。

> ▸ 你们成为了真正的生命。

> **昔涟：**但翁法罗斯已经拥有了「明天」。所以，只要开拓者悉心保留、照看所有的种子。在未来的某一天，当「开拓」再度创造奇迹后……

**Cyrene:** I believe this world we deeply love will sprout and bloom into a beautiful flower.

> **昔涟：**我相信，这个我们深爱的世界就能发芽，绽放美丽的花。

**Cyrene:** Don't worry. I've already kept all the seeds safe in "The First Fruit" for you...

> **昔涟：**别担心，人家已经帮你把所有种子都收在了「最初的果实」里……

**Cyrene:** And its name is As I've Written ♪

> **昔涟：**而它的名字，就叫《如我所书》♪

**(Trailblazer):** ...

> **开拓者：**……

**Cyrene:** So, it's time to reveal the correct answer. This isn't a dream, nor a fantasy. It's...

**Cyrene:** The epilogue of As I've Written.

> **昔涟：**《如我所书》的尾声。

**Cyrene:** At the end of the story, the characters take their final bow and embark on a journey of no return.

> **昔涟：**故事的最后，剧中人完成谢幕，踏上了永不回头的旅途。

**Cyrene:** The one who wasn't part of the story, yet helped write its romantic ending... He/She bid farewell to his/her friends and returned to his/her home beyond the tale.

> **昔涟：**那本不属于剧中，却帮助写下了浪漫结局的人儿…他/她同朋友们纷纷告别，回到属于他/她故事之外的家园。

**Cyrene:** Promise me one last thing, (Trailblazer)...

> **昔涟：**答应我最后一件事吧，开拓者……

**Cyrene:** When we say goodbye, give me a smile, okay?

> **昔涟：**说再见的时候，要笑一笑，好吗？

- ▸ I... promise you.

> ▸ 我…答应你。

> ▸ 银河绝对不会遗忘翁法罗斯。

**March 7th:** Hey, it's not fair that you get to be the only cool one here!

> **三月七：**只有你一个人耍帅，未免太犯规了吧？

> **三月七：**我们会监督他/她，必须信守承诺！

**Dan Heng:** Leave it to us, Cyrene. Your story will be sung across many worlds.

> **丹恒：**交给我们吧，昔涟。你们的故事，会被许多世界传唱。

**Cyrene:** Stories this complex and twisted are always hard to believe. If anyone questions the truth of this tale...

> **昔涟：**太过艰难曲折的故事总会令人难以相信。要是有人质疑这段故事的真实性……

**Cyrene:** You'll all have to stand up for us ♪

> **昔涟：**各位可要为我们所有人挺身而出呀♪

**March 7th:** Rest assured! Even the most stubborn person can't withstand our six fists.

> **三月七：**放一百个心吧！嘴再硬的人，也扛不住我们六只拳头。

**Cyrene:** You're so reliable, March.

> **昔涟：**真是可靠呀，三月。

**Cyrene:** Shh, listen...

> **昔涟：**嘘，你们听——

> **瓦尔特：**「…这里，留下了一本书？」

**Sunday:** "Its composition is similar to 'memoria', but it's far more resilient than ordinary Memory Bubbles."

> **星期日：**「它的构成和『忆质』近似，但远比一般的忆泡强韧得多。」

**Himeko:** "Keep it safe. Perhaps this is the final gift that Amphoreus left for the cosmos."

> **姬子：**「收好它吧。也许，它就是翁法罗斯留给银河的最后一件馈赠。」

**Pom-Pom:** "Oh no! Passenger March is sleepwalking!"

> **帕姆：**「不、不好了帕！三月乘客，正在梦游帕！」

**March 7th:** S-sleepwalking?! Oh no, oh no, I don't want to make a fool of myself in front of everyone...

> **三月七：**梦、梦游？！糟糕糟糕，我可不想在大家面前出洋相啊……

**March 7th:** ...I can hear all those sneaky little plans brewing in your head!

**March 7th:** ...Dream on!

> **三月七：**…想得美！

**Cyrene:** March, Dan Heng, (Trailblazer)...

> **昔涟：**三月，丹恒，开拓者……

**Cyrene:** In the countless days and nights ahead, you must continue to support each other.

> **昔涟：**未来的无数个日夜，你们可要继续担当彼此最宝贵的依靠呀。

### Bid farewell to this epic journey through Amphoreus

> *According to Castorice and Cipher, the Chrysos Heirs appeared on the Astral Express after the great battle. Perhaps this is the last chance to say goodbye before the epic tale comes to an end. Take some time to chat with your companions, for this might be their only opportunity to ever board the Astral Express.*

*(Talk to Phainon again, optional)*

**Phainon:** The Astral Express... honestly, it really feels like "home."

[Recap] Leaving this place means As I've Written will conclude here and reach its final chapter. Take one last look at your companions and make sure you have no regrets.

*(Interact with As I've Written)*

**Dan Heng:** Leaving this place means As I've Written will conclude here.

> **丹恒：**离开这里，也意味着《如我所书》将就此搁笔。

**Dan Heng:** No one will have the chance to revise it, and no one can continue writing it.

**March 7th:** Don't leave any regrets, (Trailblazer).

> **三月七：**千万别留下遗憾啊，开拓者。

[Recap] On the story's final page, you bade farewell to the Chrysos Heirs, concluding the characters' finale. Cyrene left behind a bookmark, telling you she had made a decision on her own, and if you could forgive her, you should add it to the end of As I've Written.

**Cyrene:** What magnificent words could possibly do justice to the finale of such an epic tale?

> **昔涟：**一篇轰轰烈烈的史诗，要多么华美的语句才能勾勒出它的尾声呢？

**Cyrene:** I've been pondering this while you were sleeping. Now, I finally have the answer...

> **昔涟：**你睡着的时候，人家一直在思考这件事。现在，我终于有了答案……

**Cyrene:** Let's keep it simple and sweet. In our virtual extrapolations, we've lived through epochs longer than the lifespans of many stars...

> **昔涟：**平平淡淡的就好。在虚拟的演算中，我们度过了比许多星星的寿命更为漫长的岁月……

**Cyrene:** No language in this world, no written words, could ever carry the weight of this journey. In that case, let's not be bound by the minutiae of words...

> **昔涟：**这世上没有任何一种语言，一行文字，能托得起这段旅途的重量。既然如此，就不必再囿于细微的字眼……

**Cyrene:** Those who understand its passion and romance will naturally be moved to tears by that final "." placed at the end ♪

> **昔涟：**理解它热烈和浪漫的人们，自然会为最后被点上的那枚「。」热泪盈眶♪

**Cyrene:** And now... it's time to say goodbye to our "readers."

> **昔涟：**那么，现在…到了和「读者们」道别的时候啦。

**Cyrene:** And so we reach the final page of "As I've Written" — let's not leave behind any regrets, everyone.

> **昔涟：**这便是「如我所书」的最后一页了——大家，别留下遗憾呀。

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**都到齐了呀…该由谁写下第一笔呢？

**Khaslana:** Going first is the hardest job. I don't want to make a fool of myself.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**打头阵的任务是最艰巨的，我可不想露怯啊。

**Aglaea:** How about letting the Great Imperator draft it for us?

> **阿格莱雅：**不如，就由凯撒大人为我们起稿吧？

**Cerydra:** Heh. Are you all such cowards?

**Cerydra:** Then I shall enlighten future generations, just like the thirty million cycles of Flame-Chase.

> **刻律德菈：**那就由我来启示后人吧——正如那三千万世的逐火一样。

**Cerydra:** "Without me, there is no world. Go think, observe... and conquer."

> **刻律德菈：**「无我，即无世界。去思，去见，去征服。」

...

**Cerydra:** What's wrong? Everyone's gone quiet?

> **刻律德菈：**怎么，都沉默了？

**Hyacinthia:** The Great Imperator set the bar way too high... How are we supposed to follow that up...?

> **雅辛忒丝：**凯撒大人的调子…起得也太高啦，让人好难接呀…？

> **迈德漠斯：**帝王身后，忠臣必将紧随。她强大的气场，恐怕也只有剑旗爵能衬托得起了。

**Helektra:** I should've shared a drink with you all, Dux Goldweaver.
