> **阿格莱雅：**放松些，海瑟音。

**Helektra:** ...

> **海列屈拉：**……

**Helektra:** Life is like the ocean: Tides rise and fall, and everything eventually dissolves into the waves. So, sing your heart out in every moment of your existence.

> **海列屈拉：**世事如沧海，潮起潮落，一切终会在浪中消逝。所以，请在生命的每一刻都纵情高歌吧。

**Helektra:** Whether it's a feast or the Nihility that awaits ahead... I will find its radiance and sing its praises.

> **海列屈拉：**不论等在前方的是欢宴还是虚无…我都会找到它的辉光，并将之歌颂。

**(Trailblazer):** "May the next feast be eternal."

> **开拓者：**「愿下一场宴席，永不散场。」

**Castorice:** Miss Hysilens's toast is... so poetic.

> **遐蝶：**海瑟音小姐的祝词…真唯美呢。

**Anaxagoras:** Does everyone have to be so poetic? In my opinion, we should leave some real "words of wisdom" for future generations.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**个个都要如此写意么？依我看，还是给后人留下些真正的「箴言」为好。

**Cifera:** Next up, any volunteers?

> **赛法利娅：**下一个，有没有人自告奋勇？

**Mydeimos:** I'll do it. There's no "dragging one's feet" in the Kremnoan language.

> **迈德漠斯：**我来。悬锋人的字典里没有「拖泥带水」。

**Khaslana:** Does the Kremnoan language even...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**悬锋人的字典里……

**Mydeimos:** ...HKS! Don't interrupt.

> **迈德漠斯：**…HKS！别打岔。

**Mydeimos:** "Kill the play, and games shall be born. Kill the strife, and glory shall descend."

> **迈德漠斯：**「去杀死玩耍，游戏便会诞生。去杀死纷争，荣光必然降临。」

**Khaslana:** Haha, not bad at all. But now I can rest easy. After all, the lines I prepared are way more profound than yours.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**哈哈，不赖嘛。不过，这下我就踏实了——毕竟我准备的台词，意蕴在你之上。

**Mydeimos:** Hah! If nothing else, you're certainly unmatched when it comes to singing your own praise.

> **迈德漠斯：**呵！别的不谈，你这自吹自擂的本事的确傲视群雄。

**Mydeimos:** Farewell, everyone. If we meet again, I shall once more become your steadfast support. That's a promise I won't back down from.

> **迈德漠斯：**诸位，择日再见。若能再会，我定将再度成为你们的后盾，义不容辞。

**(Trailblazer):** "Mydeimos, become king."

> **开拓者：**「迈德漠斯，加冕为王吧。」

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Tribios:** What's up, Cas?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**怎么了，小蝶？

**Castorice:** It's nothing, Lady Tribios. I just... finally realized something...

> **遐蝶：**没什么，缇里西庇俄丝女士。我只是，终于有了些感悟……

**Aglaea:** Then speak your mind and let your words be immortalized on the page, Cas.

> **阿格莱雅：**那就尽情言说，将它永远地留在纸页上吧，蝶。

**Castorice:** ...

> **遐蝶：**……

**Castorice:** "If your heart still has room to spare, share a bit of gentleness and softly cherish all the beautiful things around you."

> **遐蝶：**「若心灵仍有余裕，请稍许分出些温柔，轻抚身边美好的一切吧。」

**Hyacinthia:** That's so beautifully said, Cassie...

> **雅辛忒丝：**蝶宝…说得真好呀。

**Castorice:** Thank you, Miss Hyacine. My only small regret is that I couldn't fully embrace this world I loved so dearly. I hope those who read these words can fulfill this wish in my place.

> **遐蝶：**谢谢，风堇小姐。我唯一的小小遗憾，就是没能尽情去触碰这个深爱的世界——希望读到这段话的人们，能代我完成这个心愿。

**Castorice:** Everyone... let's meet again at the end of the west wind.

> **遐蝶：**大家——我们在西风的尽头再会吧。

**(Trailblazer):** "The place you reside is the gentle sea of flowers."

> **开拓者：**「你所在的地方，就是温柔的花海。」

**Anaxagoras:** Well then, it's my turn.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**那么，该到我了。

**Aglaea:** Take it away with your performance, Anaxagoras. This time, you won't have to worry about being interrupted.

> **阿格莱雅：**尽情表演吧，阿那克萨戈拉斯。这一次，你不用担心被打断了。

**Anaxagoras:** Heh, then I must thank you all for being such a captive audience. Now, with this golden opportunity, I shall reveal to the world my insights into the world's ultimate truth...

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**呵，那真要感谢你们捧场了。值此良机，我就将自己对世界真理的见解公之于众吧——

**Anaxagoras:** "'Truth is a solvent that dissolves all things in the world, and thus cannot objectively exist.' I have a brilliant proof for this in mind, but since we're nearing the end of the story, I shall not elaborate."

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**「『真理是溶解世界万物的溶剂，因而绝无法在客观上存在』——对此，本人心中已有绝妙的证明，但鉴于故事已临近尾声，故不再展开。」

**Khaslana:** Is this your final question for future generations?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**这是您给后人留下的最后一道课题？

**Anaxagoras:** No. This is an established fact, not up for debate. I call it Anaxagoras's Last Theorem.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**不，这是既定事实，无须再议。我称其为《阿那克萨戈拉斯的最后定理》。

**Hyacinthia:** Oh you. Still as profound as ever, professor. We've got time, you know. If you don't explain it clearly, future readers might not grasp your true meaning.

> **雅辛忒丝：**还是那么深奥呀，教授。时间还多，如果您不说明清楚，后世的读者恐怕看不懂您的真意喔？

**Anaxagoras:** The true meaning isn't important. What matters is the emotion that stirs within them when they read these words... At that moment, the theorem's mission would be complete.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**真意并不重要。重要的是，当他们看到这句话时，心中会升起何种情感——而在那一瞬间，这道定理的任务就完成了。

**Anaxagoras:** So, your lessons are far from over. But taking a little break for now wouldn't hurt.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**所以，你们的课程可远没结束。不过，暂时休憩一下也未尝不可。

**Anaxagoras:** Then... Class dismissed.

> **阿那克萨戈拉斯：**那么，下课。

**(Trailblazer):** "The seeds you've sown are already sprouting."

> **开拓者：**「你播下的种子，已经生根发芽。」

**Aglaea:** Would you mind if I write the next line?

> **阿格莱雅：**各位，介意由我续写这一笔么？

**Cifera:** Huh? I actually thought you should be the one doing the final wrap-up. But...

> **赛法利娅：**诶？我还觉得该由你来做最后的总结哪。不过……

**Tribios:** ...We'd never turn down a request from you, Agy.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**…我们永远不会拒绝你的请求呀，阿雅。

**Aglaea:** Heh... Teacher, Cifera... Thank you both.

> **阿格莱雅：**呵…吾师，赛法利娅——谢谢你们。

**Aglaea:** "Trust and selflessness are the two most beautiful garments in the world: one to gift others, one to adorn yourself."

> **阿格莱雅：**「信任与无私，是世上最美丽的两件华服：一件赠予他人，一件装扮自己。」

**Khaslana:** One's golden... and one's purple?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**一件是金色…一件是紫色？

**Aglaea:** Not being able to completely change your fashion sense is my only regret across thirty million cycles.

> **阿格莱雅：**没能彻底挽救你的审美，是我三千万世唯一抱憾之事呢。

**Aglaea:** After all this exhaustion, I just want to take a peaceful bath now. I'll see you all again at dawn.

> **阿格莱雅：**劳顿至此，我此时只想静心沐浴一番啊。黎明时再会吧，各位。

**(Trailblazer):** "Thank you for your hard work all this while."

> **开拓者：**「一直以来，辛苦了。」

**Hyacinthia:** Lady Aglaea just can't give up her favorite hobby, can she?

> **雅辛忒丝：**阿格莱雅女士，还是戒不掉她最大的爱好呢。

**Hyacinthia:** So, I guess it's my turn now?

> **雅辛忒丝：**那，也该轮到我啦？

**Cifera:** There must be many others like Miss Hyacine out there in the cosmos, helping and healing people everywhere. You've got to set a good example for them, right?

> **赛法利娅：**银河中也有很多跟风堇姑娘一样，到处救死扶伤的人吧？可得给他们立个好榜样哪。

**Hyacinthia:** Of course~

> **雅辛忒丝：**那是当然~

**Hyacinthia:** "Look up at the sky more often and smile more. Many stubborn ailments fear optimism, but it's the best medicine!"

> **雅辛忒丝：**「多望望天，多笑一笑；好多缠人的病痛，最怕乐观这剂良药！」

**Tribios:** It's so catchy. The kids will definitely love this message.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**朗朗上口，孩子们肯定会喜欢这条寄语呢。

**Hyacinthia:** When it comes to understanding children's hearts, Lady Tribios definitely knows best...

> **雅辛忒丝：**孩子们的心性，缇里西庇俄丝女士肯定最了解啦~

**Hyacinthia:** Look, the rainbow bridge is already hanging in the sky... Shall we get going, Little Ica?

> **雅辛忒丝：**彩虹桥，已经挂在天边了呀——小伊卡，我们走吧？

**Little Ica:** Doot... doot-doot!

> **小伊卡：**嘟…嘟嘟！

**(Trailblazer):** "It's you who healed the sky."

> **开拓者：**「然后，是你治愈了天空。」

**Cifera:** Mmm...

> **赛法利娅：**唔……

**Khaslana:** What's on your mind, Miss Cipher?

> **卡厄斯兰那：**在想什么，赛飞儿小姐？

**Cifera:** Hmm... I'm thinking, should I leave behind a riddle? Some twisted, convoluted scheme that'll drive everyone in the universe mad trying to crack it?

> **赛法利娅：**呼…我在想，要不要留下一句谜语？一个九折十八弯的诡计，让全宇宙的人为了挖掘它的谜底发狂？

**Khaslana:** Hahaha... I see. You know what? Just go wild with it! No one's gonna judge you.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**哈哈哈…原来如此。要我说，就尽情发挥吧？没人会责怪你的。

**Cifera:** Oh well, my brain's not working right now. Let me be genuinely sincere for once...

> **赛法利娅：**罢了罢了，脑子一时转不动了哪。就让本姑娘难得真诚一回吧——

**Cifera:** "Don't be afraid to fall: just run! Destiny's just a slow-moving nymph. It can't catch you!"

> **赛法利娅：**「别怕摔，跑起来！命运就是只迟缓的若虫，它压根抓不住你！」

**Tribios:** So in the end, Little Ciphy's message was the most straightforward one?

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**结果，小飞儿的留言是最简洁通俗的一条呢？

**Cifera:** Even if you combined 30 million of me, I probably wouldn't have read as many books as you did in one lifetime! I won't pretend to be cultured before big sis Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne...

> **赛法利娅：**三千万个我加起来，读过的书可能都没你一辈子多吧？我可不想硬装有文化哪，缇宝、缇宁、缇安阿姐~

**Cifera:** Amphoreus's greatest Trickery gotta bounce now... Meow!

> **赛法利娅：**翁法罗斯最伟大的「诡计」，先溜一步——喵！

**(Trailblazer):** "Homeward you come, distant wind."

> **开拓者：**「远行的风儿，回家吧。」

**Tribios:** ...It's just us now, Snowy.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**…只剩我们了呢，小白。

**Khaslana:** Yeah, teacher. Maybe I should...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**是啊，老师。要不，我……

**Tribios:** No. Let me go first. After all...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**不，还是我先吧。毕竟——

**Tribios:** "All shall bid farewell to one, and that person alone will..."

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**「众人将与一人离别，唯其人将……」

**Khaslana:** I never would have guessed you had such a dark sense of humor, teacher...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**真没想到，老师你还擅长黑色幽默啊……

**Tribios:** Hehe... You still know so little about the "complete" Tribios, Snowy.

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**嘻…你对「完整」的缇里西庇俄丝还知之甚少呢，小白。

**Tribios:** Jokes aside, I want to leave these words at the end of the story...

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**不开玩笑啦，我想在故事的尾声留下的话语——

**Tribios:** "Whether it's a sunny day or rainy day, remember to tell yourself before you drift off to sleep: 'See you tomorrow!'"

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**「不论晴天还是雨天，进入梦乡以前，记得和自己说一声：『明天见』！」

**Khaslana:** See you tomorrow, times a thousand.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**明天见，乘以一千。

**Tribios:** "Prophecy" received, times a thousand~

> **缇里西庇俄丝：**「预言」收到，乘以一千~

**(Trailblazer):** "From now on, no more goodbyes."

> **开拓者：**「从今以后，不再有离别。」

**Khaslana:** Finally, the time has come to say farewell.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**终于，到了该说再见的时候了。

**Khaslana:** I'm grateful that you could be here to witness this moment... partner.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**你能见证这个时刻…我很感激，搭档。

- ▸ Can we have another journey together, partner?

> ▸ 还能一起旅行吗，搭档？

**Khaslana:** Of course. As long as As I've Written stays by your side, Amphoreus will always be with you.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**当然。只要《如我所书》还在你手边，翁法罗斯就一直与你同在。

- ▸ My wish is to meet with you all again.

> ▸ 我的愿望，就是同你们再会。

**Khaslana:** Well, then make sure you take good care of As I've Written. As long as it's by your side, Amphoreus will always be with you.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**那，你一定要把《如我所书》保管好啊。只要它还在你手边，翁法罗斯就一直与你同在。

**Khaslana:** The last line I want to write down is...

> **卡厄斯兰那：**那么，我最后想写下的一行话……

**Khaslana:** "A hero is living in everyone's heart. Embrace it, and chase after the Sun."

> **卡厄斯兰那：**「每个人的心中都住着一个英雄。拥抱它，然后去追逐太阳吧。」

**Khaslana:** I hope those who read this will find the courage to face their burning lives.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**希望读到它的人，都能鼓起勇气面对燃烧的生命。

**Khaslana:** See you at the next pale dawn, partner.

> **卡厄斯兰那：**下个灰白色的黎明再会吧，搭档。

**(Trailblazer):** "Did you see? Amphoreus has ushered in the dawn."

> **开拓者：**「你看见了吗？翁法罗斯终于迎来了黎明。」

**Dan Heng:** The characters in the book have taken their final bow. It's time for us... to return to our own reality.

> **丹恒：**书中人的谢幕已经完成了。我们…也该回到属于我们的现实了。

**March 7th:** But as people who lived through it, we can still add the final touches to this story, right?

> **三月七：**不过，作为亲历者，我们还是可以给这个故事添上最后的几笔，对吧？

**Dan Heng:** Of course.

> **丹恒：**嗯，当然。

**Dan Heng:** "The pen will eventually break, the ink will run dry, and life will ultimately fade, but thoughts will permanently remain in sagas."

> **丹恒：**「笔终会折断，墨终会耗尽，生命终会逝去，但思想将在史诗中永垂不朽。」

**March 7th:** "If time is an endless cycle, then forget the bitterness that keeps repeating, and remember only the moments of happiness!"

> **三月七：**「如果岁月是个无穷的轮回——那就忘掉不断重复的苦涩，单独记住每个快乐的瞬间吧！」

**(Trailblazer):** "Let's walk towards tomorrow together! We were never apart."

> **开拓者：**「一起走向明天，我们不曾分离。」

**Dan Heng:** Time to head home, (Trailblazer).

> **丹恒：**该回家了，开拓者。

**March 7th:** Don't miss the Crew meeting! We won't wait around for you, you know?

> **三月七：**可别错过了列车组的会议！我们可不会特意等你哦？

**Cyrene:** See you later, Dan Heng, March...

> **昔涟：**再见啦，丹恒，三月~

**Cyrene:** And with this, As I've Written officially comes to an end.

> **昔涟：**到了这里，《如我所书》就算正式完本啦。

- ▸ Shouldn't I leave something behind?

> ▸ 我不需要留下些什么吗？

- ▸ Aren't you... Going to say something?

> ▸ 你…不说些什么吗？

**Cyrene:** We're its co-authors after all, (Trailblazer).

> **昔涟：**我们可是它共同的主笔呀，开拓者。

**Cyrene:** Once the words are written down and have been read by others, the meaning they carry is no longer in our control.

> **昔涟：**文字脱离了作者的手笔，被他人收入眼中的时候，它承载的意义就不受你我掌控啦。

**Cyrene:** Leaving the freedom of interpretation to every reader who opens it... Don't you think there's something romantic about that?

> **昔涟：**把解读的自由留给每一位翻开它的读者…不觉得这是件很浪漫的事吗？

**Cyrene:** As for what this story means to you and me... Let's keep it close to our hearts.

> **昔涟：**至于这段故事在你我眼中的意义，就让我们把它好好放在心底吧……

**Cyrene:** After all, we've always had this unspoken understanding between us ♪

> **昔涟：**反正，人家一直都和你心照不宣呀♪

**Pom-Pom:** ...Whoa! P—Passenger March, why did you suddenly wake up? You scared me so much that I dropped that book!

> **帕姆：**…哇！三、三月乘客，你怎么突然醒来了帕！吓得我，都把书掉到地上了！

**Himeko:** Welcome home, you three. Thank you for your hard work on this "Trailblaze."

> **姬子：**欢迎回家，三位。这次的「开拓」，也辛苦你们了。

**Welt:** We might need to reshuffle the seniority rankings of the Express Crew if we count the years you spent in Amphoreus.

> **瓦尔特：**算上你们在翁法罗斯渡过的年月，列车组的辈分怕是该重新排布了。

**March 7th:** Hey, I was the first one to get dragged into it! Does that mean I'm gonna be the oldest member of the Astral Express now?

> **三月七：**啊，我可是第一个被卷进去的！这么说来，我要变成星穹列车的大姐大啦？

**Sunday:** Miss March hasn't lost her innocent nature even after all these long years.

> **星期日：**即便经历了漫长岁月的洗礼，三月小姐还是没有丢下纯真的本质。

**Dan Heng:** In other words, she hasn't really grown up at all.

> **丹恒：**转译一下，就是没有什么实质性的长进。

**March 7th:** ...Hey!

> **三月七：**——喂！

**March 7th:** This is the first time I've been able to examine As I've Written up close...

> **三月七：**这还是，我第一次离得这么近端详这本《如我所书》……

**Dan Heng:** Miss Cyrene's handwriting is gentle yet steely... with an underlying strength.

> **丹恒：**昔涟小姐的笔迹…绵柔中透着刚韧。

**March 7th:** ...I had no idea it was so sophisticated. Looks like I need to brush up on my knowledge in this area.

> **三月七：**…原来这么有讲究吗？看来，我也该恶补一下这方面的知识了。

**(Trailblazer):** Maybe start with a few basic books first to get your feet wet.

> **开拓者：**你还是先随便找几本书入个门吧。

**March 7th:** Look who's talking! As if you're not glued to your phone in your room all day!

> **三月七：**说得好像你不是每天都躲在房间里刷手机似的！

**March 7th:** ...Huh? Wait. Something fell out...

> **三月七：**…欸？等等，有什么东西掉出来了——

**Dan Heng:** It's...

> **丹恒：**这是……

**March 7th:** Is that... a bookmark?

> **三月七：**是…书签吗？

**Cyrene:** In a corner unknown to everyone, there was actually an untold "interlude" that took place.

> **昔涟：**其实，在大家不知道的角落，还发生了一段鲜有人知的「插曲」。

**Cyrene:** I left it out of the story because it involved a "choice"... A choice I made for my own, selfish reasons.

> **昔涟：**我将它留在了故事之外，因为它事关一个「选择」，一个我出于私心而做的选择。

**Cyrene:** Can you forgive me, (Trailblazer)? I made a decision on my own while you were away...

> **昔涟：**可以原谅我吗，开拓者。你不在场的时候，我擅自做了一个决定……

**Cyrene:** I wrote everything about it on this little card. If you can find it in your heart to forgive me after reading it...

> **昔涟：**我把关于它的一切都写在了这张小卡片上。读过以后，假如你愿意原谅我——

**Cyrene:** Please, add this final note to the conclusion of As I've Written.

> **昔涟：**就把这最后一笔，添在《如我所书》的结尾吧？

### Cyrene's choice after the fall of Irontomb

> *Irontomb has been defeated, but the story of Amphoreus, The Eternal Land, is not yet over. Or rather, how it should end depends on Cyrene's choice...*

[Recap] After the fall of Irontomb, Cyrene encountered Herta in the Path Space.

Moments after the fall of Irontomb...

**Cyrene:** Memories swallowed by Irontomb, return now to reality.

> **昔涟：**被铁墓吞没的记忆，回到现实吧。

**Cyrene:** And don't lose your way ♪

> **昔涟：**可千万不要迷路呀♪

**Herta:** I've finally found you, Cyrene... Demiurge.

> **黑塔：**终于找到你了，昔涟…德谬歌。

[Recap] The Aeon of Remembrance had not yet been born. THEY will only ascend to godhood at the end of time, and THEIR "memory" will stretch through the past, becoming what people now call "Fuli." As a Pure Child of Anāsrava who could potentially become Fuli, Cyrene had two choices: either escape Amphoreus and journey toward the stars, or offer herself as a sacrifice and draw a close to the cycle of causality in Amphoreus, becoming the "memory" that pierced through the world.

**Cyrene:** We won the battle. Thank you, Madam Herta. Without you, we never would've stood a chance.

> **昔涟：**我们赢下了这场战役呢。感谢您，黑塔女士。如果没有您，胜利根本无从谈起。

**Cyrene:** Still, is something weighing on you?

> **昔涟：**只是，还有什么后顾之忧吗？

**Herta:** Right before I shut my eyes, inside the Droidhead's brain, I saw... something unsettling.

> **黑塔：**合上眼睛前，在机器头的脑袋里，我看见了…一些令人在意的画面。

**Herta:** The Fourth Instant, this turning point in cosmic destiny, anchored by the Erudition... It's not a single instant, but a process, a chain of events.

> **黑塔：**「第四时刻」——由「智识」锚定的，银河命运的转折点…它并非某个瞬间，而是一段历程，许多事件的集合。

**Cyrene:** So, this victory isn't the final piece of that chain?

> **昔涟：**所以，这场胜利不是那「许多事件」的最后一环？

**Herta:** Far from it. This is only the beginning...

> **黑塔：**恰恰相反，它只是开始……

**Herta:** The first toll of the death knell for the cosmos.

> **黑塔：**是这片银河的第一声丧钟。

**Cyrene:** That sorrowful look doesn't suit you, Madam Herta... Don't hold it in. Tell me.

> **昔涟：**这幅惆怅的表情可不适合您呀，黑塔女士…别有负担，告诉我吧？

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** Not long ago, you crossed paths with the Aeon of Remembrance again, didn't you?

> **黑塔：**不久前，你再次遇见了「记忆」星神，对么？

**Cyrene:** Yes. Even if the figure was hazy, I knew right away: That was the god from "Cyrene's" dreams.

> **昔涟：**嗯。哪怕身形模糊不清，人家还是确信：那就是「昔涟」梦中的神明。

**Herta:** "Causality" has gone wrong... just as I thought.

> **黑塔：**果然…「因果」出现问题了。

**Cyrene:** Causality?

> **昔涟：**因果？

**Herta:** Let me put it in your favorite fairy-tale terms. The universe began as a seed. It grew into a great tree, and the "present" is its branches and leaves.

> **黑塔：**用你最爱的童话作比喻吧：宇宙起源于一枚种子，它长成大树，「现在」是它的枝叶。

**Herta:** The Erudition calculates how the "future" will sprout. With so many possible outcomes, THEY choose to "anchor" one path, cutting away the crooked or fragile branches.

> **黑塔：**「智识」会计算「未来」如何发芽。当然，未来有无数种发展，所以祂需要「锚定」，剪除那些长歪的、不够健壮的枝叶，只留下一种可能。

**Herta:** But if things continue that way, the branches grow endlessly, the leaves multiply, and one day the tree collapses under its own weight.

> **黑塔：**但如果只是这样，树枝越长越长，叶片越来越多，树迟早有一天会不堪重负。

**Herta:** That is why there is a concept in biology called "lignification." Soft sprigs form a hardened shell and fuse with the trunk.

> **黑塔：**所以，生物学上有种说法叫「木质化」，让嫩枝生出坚硬的表皮，与树干连为一体。

**Herta:** For the universe, that process is what we call "memory."

> **黑塔：**对于宇宙，这就是「记忆」的本质。

**Herta:** Under Fuli's record-keeping, the "past" becomes "memory." In the corners THEY forget, even the events of the "present" become fruit without roots.

> **黑塔：**在浮黎的铭记下，「过去」凝结成「记忆」；而祂遗忘的角落，即便有「现在」发生，也会成为无根之果。

**Cyrene:** So it is the opposite of the Erudition... One calculates what is to come, and the other holds on to what came before.

> **昔涟：**听起来，和「智识」正相反呢…一位计算未来，一位铭记过去。

**Herta:** We were never meant to have to speak of this. But now I finally understand what that chaotic matter around Amphoreus truly is...

> **黑塔：**原本我们不用谈论此事。但事到如今，我终于明白了包裹翁法罗斯的那团混沌物质是什么……

**Herta:** It is your "memory." Your mind now stretches across the stars, allowing you to travel farther down this Path than anyone else.

> **黑塔：**那是你的「记忆」。现在，你的心识遍布群星，让你在这条命途上走出了极其遥远的距离。

**Cyrene:** My... "memory"?

> **昔涟：**我的…「记忆」？

**Herta:** Yes. You live in the "now," but your "memory" remains in the past.

> **黑塔：**对。虽然你活在「当下」，但你的「记忆」存在于过去。

**Herta:** Just like the Aeon of Remembrance, a being made entirely of memories.

> **黑塔：**就像「记忆」的星神，也只是一簇记忆。

**Cyrene:** What... does that mean?

> **昔涟：**这是…什么意思？

**Cyrene:** Did THEY fall?

> **昔涟：**祂，陨落了吗？

**Herta:** No. Quite the opposite. THEY have not yet been born.

> **黑塔：**不。恰恰相反，是祂还未诞生。

**Herta:** Remember? To carry out the mission of "remembering" the universe, THEY must ascend to divinity only at the very end of time.

> **黑塔：**记得么？如果要贯彻「铭记」宇宙的信念，祂只会在时间的尽头飞升成神。

**Herta:** And at that moment of ascension, THEIR "memory" stretches through the past, becoming what people now call "Fuli."

> **黑塔：**而飞升的那一刻，祂的「记忆」贯穿过去，成为了人们眼中的「浮黎」。

**Cyrene:** Ah...

> **昔涟：**啊……

**Herta:** That is why THEY feel so distant, so cold. That crystalline form is just an empty shell. I do not know how the Garden of Recollection managed to hide this until now. They must have their reasons. But none of that matters now.

> **黑塔：**所以祂才那么不近人情，那水晶身子只是一具空壳。我不知道忆庭怎么能把这件事瞒到现在——他们有理由这么做——但这些都不重要。

**Herta:** Here in the present, we cannot know who will become Fuli, but one thing is certain: THEY will be born.

> **黑塔：**身处「当下」的我们，没法弄清是谁成为了浮黎，但能确信祂一定会诞生。

**Herta:** And those who might become Fuli all share the same characteristics...

> **黑塔：**而那些可能成为浮黎的人，具备相同的特征——

**Herta:** They are known as children of Remembrance. Pure Children of Anāsrava.

> **黑塔：**她们被称作「记忆」的孩子，无漏净子。

Memokeeper's Embodiment: Both of you... children of Remembrance...

**March 7th?:** So, help us. Find where Amphoreus's "memory" began... and bring it out.

> **三月七？：**所以，帮帮我们，找到翁法罗斯「记忆」的起点，然后把它带出来……

**Terrified Memosnatcher:** "We. Are the sacrifice. The Garden. Used us. The 'Pure Child of Anāsrava.' Abandoned us."

> **恐惧的窃忆者：**「我们。是牺牲品。忆庭。利用了我们。『无漏净子』。抛弃了我们。」

**Cyrene:** So that name... it was never my "past"...

> **昔涟：**原来，这个名字背后不是人家的「过去」……

**Cyrene:** It was my "future."

> **昔涟：**而是我的「未来」呀。

**Herta:** Or maybe, it is the "present."

> **黑塔：**也可能，是「现在」。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Herta:** Nous... has revealed to me two paths.

> **黑塔：**博识尊…为我揭示了两条路。

**Herta:** And on both paths, I see you standing.

> **黑塔：**那两条路上都站着你。

**Herta:** The first: Follow the trail of "memory" you have forged, break free from this world, and reach for the stars.

> **黑塔：**一、沿着这条你踏出的「记忆」之路，挣脱这个世界，走向群星。

**Herta:** That would mean Amphoreus's causality will no longer concern you, at least not the person you are now.

> **黑塔：**这也意味着，翁法罗斯的因果与你无关，至少和现在的你无关。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** Go on.

> **昔涟：**请说下去吧。

**Herta:** The second...

> **黑塔：**二……

**Herta:** You come to a stop and "fade," offering yourself... to become the "memory" that carries through Amphoreus.

> **黑塔：**你停下脚步，以自我为代价完成「收梢」…成为贯穿翁法罗斯的「记忆」。

**Herta:** Perhaps this is the choice every Pure Child of Anāsrava must face.

> **黑塔：**这或许是每一个无漏净子都要面临的抉择。

**Cyrene:** That choice means I'll remain in the "past" forever.

> **昔涟：**这意味着，我会永远留在「过去」。

**Herta:** And more than that, it means you will close off Amphoreus's causality. This world is sealed. The effect that the Remembrance brings to it will all converge on one thing. That one thing is "you."

> **黑塔：**不仅如此，你会闭合翁法罗斯的因果。这是一个封闭世界，所以「记忆」为它带来的影响，会全部收束为「你」的影响。

**Herta:** No more Aeons will interfere with this part of history. No more "blank spaces" in causality. The "present" will be set in stone and become indestructible.

> **黑塔：**不会再有星神介入这段历史，也不会再有因果的「空白」，于是「现在」会被固定下来，变得坚不可摧。

**Herta:** There are two paths, and only one leads to the right end. And Droidhead didn't tell me the answer.

> **黑塔：**两条路，只有一条是对的。机器头没有告诉我答案。

**Herta:** But I can guess what might happen if you choose wrong. With "causality incomplete," there is a chance Irontomb could rise again.

> **黑塔：**但错误的后果可以想象——因为「因果缺失」，有一定概率，铁墓会卷土重来。

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** So even the gods of the cosmos are unreasonable.

> **昔涟：**银河里的神明，也是这么不讲道理呢。

**Herta:** If you want my advice, stop thinking so much. Ask yourself what your heart truly wants.

> **黑塔：**我的建议：别想那么多，先问问自己的内心。

**Herta:** Choose the first path, and you might survive as a memosprite. You may even walk beside them for a while.

> **黑塔：**选第一条路，你或许能以忆灵的形式存在下去，甚至和他们同行一段时间。

**Herta:** Even I can tell how much they mean to you, especially (Trailblazer).

> **黑塔：**哪怕是我也看得出来，你很在意他们，尤其是开拓者。

**Herta:** But if you take the second one...

> **黑塔：**但如果选第二条路……

**Herta:** Then you may have already lost the chance to say goodbye.

> **黑塔：**那你可能已经错过告别的机会了。

[Recap] Cyrene chose to look back at the past. She believed that you, the one she cared about most, would surely witness every starlit tomorrow for her.

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** I have made my decision, Madam Herta.

> **昔涟：**我想好啦，黑塔女士。

**Herta:** I thought you would need time to think it through.

> **黑塔：**我以为你需要时间仔细斟酌。

**Cyrene:** The answer is clear, is it not? Still, I do need a little time...

> **昔涟：**答案显而易见，不是吗？但人家确实需要一点时间……

**Cyrene:** To quiet the sorrow in my heart.

> **昔涟：**来克服心底的哀伤。

**Cyrene:** His/Her kindness is always just right for the moment. I believe... he/she will forgive me?

> **昔涟：**他/她的温柔总是恰到好处，一定…会原谅我吧？

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** I will say no more. I would rather not break the mood.

> **黑塔：**我就不多说什么了，免得破坏气氛。

**Herta:** But may I ask you one last thing?

> **黑塔：**但最后，可以再问你一个问题吗？

**Cyrene:** Of course.

> **昔涟：**当然。

**Herta:** Why?

> **黑塔：**为什么？

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** I once heard a saying that went something like this: Divinity is "flawless humanity," and humanity is "flawed divinity"...

> **昔涟：**我听过这么一种说法：神性是「无瑕的人性」，而人性是「有瑕的神性」……

**Cyrene:** We children of Amphoreus gave up on gods long ago. Those beings who love to play pranks on the universe...

> **昔涟：**翁法罗斯的孩子，早就不相信神明啦。比起喜欢恶作剧的祂们……

**Cyrene:** I believe in him/her much more than I believe in THEM. And I believe in who I am, as a "human."

> **昔涟：**我肯定更相信他/她，还有身为「人」的自己。

**Herta:** ...

> **黑塔：**……

**Herta:** That really is... a comforting answer.

> **黑塔：**真是个…让人安心的答案。

**Cyrene:** As for the one wish I still have trouble letting go of...

> **昔涟：**至于我最难割舍的那份私心……

**Cyrene:** One will remain in the past...

> **昔涟：**一人留在过去……

**Cyrene:** One will walk toward the future.

> **昔涟：**一人走向未来。

**Cyrene:** I believe that the one I cared for most...

> **昔涟：**我相信，那个我最在乎的他/她……

**Cyrene:** Will witness on behalf of the Ripples of Past Reverie...

> **昔涟：**一定会替「往昔的涟漪」见证……

**Cyrene:** Every starlit tomorrow ♪

> **昔涟：**每一个星星闪耀的明天♪

[Recap] Cyrene returned to the moment when Irontomb destroyed the world, and created An Eternal Page. It seemed Fuli hadn't been the one glancing at Amphoreus, but rather Cyrene looking back into the past.

**Cyrene:** Moving backwards through "remembrance"... What a curious feeling.

> **昔涟：**在「记忆」中逆行，真是新奇的体验呀。

**Cyrene:** Is this the moment Irontomb declares victory and casts the Destruction upon the stars?

> **昔涟：**这一刻，是「铁墓」宣告胜利，将「毁灭」洒向群星的瞬间？

**Cyrene:** So back then, in the Path Space, it was "I" who found "me." The god's whispers were nothing more than a girl talking to herself.

> **昔涟：**原来，那时在命途狭间，是「我」找到了「我」。神明的耳语，竟然是少女的自言自语呀。

**Cyrene:** Now that I remember, what I heard back then was...

> **昔涟：**回想起来，当时听见的话是……

you/i were/weren't once progeny of deities cast/fallen to the mortal realm for blessings/sins the verses written/erased by your/my hands were/shall be your/my remembrance/oblivion in the eden of blessed insight.

**Cyrene:** All I wanted to say was, "Don't give up. (Trailblazer) will find you"... "Memory" truly is amazing.

> **昔涟：**可我明明只是想说：「别放弃，开拓者会找到你」…「记忆」可真是奇妙呀。

**Cyrene:** Then let me wield this power...

> **昔涟：**那，就让人家行使这份力量……

**Cyrene:** And plant the seed of causality for the "miracle" that is destined to bloom ♪

> **昔涟：**为注定发生的「奇迹」，种下因果吧♪

- ▸ Let the ripples of causality spread...

> ▸ 播下因果的涟漪——

- ▸ Create An Eternal Page.

> ▸ 创造「一页永恒」。

## 164　Fallen Petals, Leave Fading Traces of Fragrance　落英啊，残芳纷飞留归躅

> 世界：Amphoreus　|　区域：Beyond Time: An Eternal Page　|　角色：Cyrene; Trailblazer; Dan Heng; Mem; March 7th; Himeko; Black Swan; Phainon; Fuli; Livia; Piso; Mydei; Anaxa; Diotima; Castorice; Polyxia; Hyacine; Cipher; Aglaea; Tribios; Hysilens; Cerydra; Pom-Pom; "Shush"; Sunday; Himeko; Tribbie; Little Ica　|　前置：Trailblaze Mission ''Silver Chariot, Part With That Humanity's Epic'' completed

### Depart from the eternal page to embark on a journey to look back into the past

> *If the world's causality were a stream flowing downstream, then the girl would be a flower, blown against the current by the winds of time. Following her backward gaze, she tiptoes across the water, casting ripples, completing the cycle of cause and effect, and leave a lingering fragrance. Engraving every precious moment here, she gradually draws closer... to that very beginning.*

[Recap] In the final story, Cyrene journeyed with the "ripples of past reverie." She followed the stream of memories upward, connecting all those moments in which the gods made themselves known.

**Cyrene:** Even so, must we walk all the way to the very beginning of "memory"?

> **昔涟：**话虽如此，要一直走到「记忆」的起点吗？

**Cyrene:** It's such a long road. A bit lonely to walk alone.

> **昔涟：**这么远的路，一个人多少有些寂寞呀。

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Then, would it be all right if I came with you?

> **往昔的涟漪：**那，介意多一个人和你同行吗？

**Cyrene:** ...

> **昔涟：**……

**Cyrene:** As long as it's Peach, even thirty million wouldn't be too many.

> **昔涟：**如果是桃子，多三千万个都不介意哦？

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** One is enough.

> **往昔的涟漪：**一个就够啦。

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Take a deep breath, and then we can set off, alright?

> **往昔的涟漪：**收拾好心情，我们准备出发吧？

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Follow the "Ripples of Remembrance" into the past. Reconnect every moment in which "the gods made themselves known."

> **往昔的涟漪：**沿着「记忆的涟漪」走向过去，将那些「神明显灵」的时刻一一串联起来。

**Cyrene:** Clearly, "cause" leads to "effect."

> **昔涟：**明明是「因」促成了「果」。

**Cyrene:** But now, the future gives new meaning to the past. "Effect" flows backward and nourishes "cause"...

> **昔涟：**但现在，却是「未来」赋予了「过去」新的意义，「果」反过来滋养了「因」……

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Maybe that is what makes memory so beautiful ♪

> **往昔的涟漪：**也许，这就是「记忆」的美妙之处啦♪

### Cast ripples and initiate the first gaze backward

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** So let us return the way we came.

> **往昔的涟漪：**所以，沿着来时路，出发吧。

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Look back at every ripple that formed. Go back home.

> **往昔的涟漪：**回望组成了每一道涟漪。回家。

[Recap] In the moment the car crashed, Cyrene lifted your body with a gentle gaze. When you appeared before Oronyx, even the Time Titan recognized Cyrene as Fuli the Remembrance.

> **记忆中的开拓者：**……

> **记忆中的丹恒：**怎么可能？！呼吸……

**Dan Heng (Memory):** (Trailblazer)... (Trailblazer)...!

> **记忆中的丹恒：**开拓者…开拓者…！

**Dan Heng (Memory):** No... I have to get you out of here first... Just hold on...!

> **记忆中的丹恒：**不行…得先带你出去…坚持住…！

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Remember? That pale dawn breaking through the endless night...

> **往昔的涟漪：**还记得吗？灰白的黎明，刺破长夜的瞬间……

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** The spear of Strife nearly claimed (Trailblazer)'s life...

> **往昔的涟漪：**「纷争」的长矛险些夺走了开拓者的生命——

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** And it was you — you, "playing" god — who cradled his/her body with your gentle gaze.

> **往昔的涟漪：**是你——「冒充」神明的你——用温柔的目光托起他/她的形体。

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Then, before Oronyx's altar...

> **往昔的涟漪：**然后，在欧洛尼斯的祭坛前……

**Oronyx:** Such a splendid memory...

> **欧洛尼斯：**多么绚烂的记忆……

> **欧洛尼斯：**天父…你在看吗？

**Ripples of Past Reverie:** Even the Titan of Time mistook you for someone else.

> **往昔的涟漪：**就连「岁月」的泰坦，都认错了你的面貌呀。

**Cyrene:** I never imagined there was a hidden truth behind our first meeting.

> **昔涟：**真没想到，我们的初遇还藏着这一层秘密呢。
